# 23: Chapter 23: Abomination Gym!

Ilene was evidently a bit confused, but Dylan didn't explain much.

The reason Ekans could evolve into Arbok so quickly was due to the level-up cheat of the growth simulation, something he obviously couldn't share with Ilene.

Dylan believed his talent as a trainer wasn't bad, but he wasn't exactly sure how good it was.

After all, he was a man blessed with the power of Viridian.

Who are the people blessed with the power of Viridian?

In the special edition, there's Giovanni, Lance, and then Yellow.

According to rumors, every ten years or several decades, a person with special abilities is born in Viridian, and every person chosen by Viridian is extremely unique.

Giovanni and Lance need no introduction. In the later stages of the special edition, they could even hold off one of the three deities of Sinnoh in the Sinjoh Ruins, facing the deity head-on and successfully delaying it. This achievement is absolutely top-tier among champions.

Yellow is even more special. She not only possesses the basic abilities of the Viridian power, like healing and sensing Pokémon's thoughts, but also, as her fighting spirit rises, her Viridian power can temporarily enhance her Pokémon's strength level!

She can cheat for her Pokémon on the spot, how about that?

This special Viridian power is unique to Yellow; Giovanni and Lance don't have this ability.

So, it's proven that no one chosen by Viridian is weak.

As for Dylan, he hadn't found anything particularly special about his Viridian power yet. It seemed to only have the abilities to heal Pokémon and sense their emotions, along with a natural affinity with Pokémon.

Yes, Dylan mainly relied on this 'affinity,' barely using his healing ability.

…

A few more days passed.

"Ah! Hitmonlee!"

In the Rota Gym, amidst the heart-wrenching cries of the challenger, a heavily poisoned Hitmonlee fell.

Releasing its fangs still shimmering with a dark purple poison glow, Arbok flicked its tongue and slowly slithered back to Dylan.

"Hitmonlee is unable to battle. Arbok wins. Since challenger Koichi's two Pokémon are both unable to battle..."

"The winner of this match is Gym Leader Dylan."

The referee uncle raised the flag, announcing the battle's result.

"Darn it! So close!"

The muscular Koichi, dressed in a karate uniform, gritted his teeth in frustration.

"…"

As Dylan walked over to say a few words, his eyelids twitched, glancing at Arbok, which was almost spotless, and fell into deep thought.

If he remembered correctly, Hitmonlee didn't even touch Arbok during the battle.

"I'm sorry, but you didn't defeat me, so I can't give you the Rota Badge for now."

"During the battle, you were too emotional, making many wrong decisions, putting your Pokémon at more risk."

"Maybe you should work on honing your patience."

Dylan smiled, walking up to the glaring Koichi and speaking slowly.

Koichi was taken aback, then recalled that he indeed made several overly aggressive mistakes during the battle, causing his Hitmonchan and Hitmonlee to fall behind.

But...

Koichi's expression turned grim. He couldn't bring himself to admit his mistakes, as he was straightforward and proud by nature.

Especially not to this guy!

As a fighter who revered Fighting-types and direct confrontation, he despised Ghost-type trainers who relied on tricks the most!

Those are abominations!

As a trainer, instead of thinking about how to use strong attacks to crush opponents head-on, they resort to flashy tricks.

Whenever Koichi encountered those Ghost-type specialist trainers, he would spit before walking away.

Teaching them a lesson? Hmph, as a noble Fighting-type trainer, why bother with those Ghost-type trainers?

Let them rot on the blacklist!

But now, Koichi felt he had to add another type to his blacklist.

"A gym without any trainer spirit! I don't want this badge anyway!"

Retrieving his Hitmonlee, Koichi disdainfully replied and turned to leave.

Damn it, what a lousy gym! A grown man using such despicable poison tactics!

Thinking of how his Hitmonchan and Hitmonlee were poisoned during the battle, Koichi felt like he was going to explode.

He wanted to use his years of karate training to fight this frail-looking gym leader, but reason told him that Officer Jenny's silver bracelets were quite sturdy, and jail food wasn't tasty.

Yes, he had tried.

So, he had only one thing to say to Dylan.

"Abomination gym!!! I'll never come back!"

"…"

Watching the burly Koichi's retreating figure, Dylan tilted his head. Why was this guy so fragile? And he called him an abomination?

"Mr. Koichi."

Dylan seemed to recall something.

"?"

Koichi turned around with a sour expression.

"Before the challenge, we agreed that if you didn't succeed, you would leave a good review for my gym."

Dylan's smile was clean and harmless.

"You!"

Koichi's face turned red instantly. This abomination! After tormenting him, he still wanted a good review?

"Mr. Koichi?"

Seeing Koichi silent and staring at him, Dylan asked again with a puzzled look.

Koichi's face turned green!

"…Fine!"

Koichi squeezed out the word through gritted teeth and then quickly walked out of the gym without looking back.

Damn!

He felt like he must have had a brain freeze, seeing that notice board and impulsively challenging the gym.

Before coming, he even mocked the idea, thinking who would advertise a gym so ostentatiously? It was the first time he saw an advertisement at the town entrance, formatted like a notice board. Interesting.

And then…

But it didn't matter! At least he tested the waters for his comrades!

Walking out of the gym, Koichi took out his phone and entered a group chat consisting of many Fighting-type trainers, including those who practiced karate and martial arts.

Seeing several people had already tagged him, asking about the results, Koichi felt a wave of embarrassment.

Before entering the Rota Gym, he had bragged about showing this gym leader the true spirit of a Fighting-type trainer!

But now… it was a bit awkward.

Inside the Rota Gym.

"Pfft~ That big guy's face kept turning red and green, so funny!"

The receptionist girl laughed, finding Koichi's expressions too amusing.

"The challengers these past few days seem quite inexperienced."

Dylan shrugged. The notice board was effective; at least the number of challengers had noticeably increased in the past few days.

Yes, Koichi was already the third challenger today.

Moreover, Dylan noticed that even if he won, some challengers wouldn't leave good reviews.

For instance, a self-proclaimed elite trainer left a bad review after losing, clearly unable to handle defeat.

'Terrible review! The gym leader lacks any trainer spirit, using Toxic Spikes and stalling for time. How can such a person be a gym leader? Disgusting! Disgusting!!'

Seeing this obviously salty review, Dylan just smiled.

After this incident, Dylan realized that getting good reviews needed to be agreed upon before the battle. Unless the challenger was extremely unsporting, they would honor their promise and leave a good review, even if the battle left them mentally exhausted.

With good reviews, more people would come to challenge, creating a positive cycle.

Although some recent reviews seemed a bit strange.

"How to say... Gym Leader Dylan is warm and sunny, and he pointed out my shortcomings. I highly recommend this gym, definitely worth a try!"

"Good review. Next time, I'll bring Steel-type Pokémon! Can someone tell me where to catch them?"

"Ugh… Gym Leader Dylan is very handsome, but the difficulty is too high! My head was spinning after the gym battle."

"Even though I didn't win, I acknowledge the gym leader's challenge level. But I still refuse to accept it! Abomination gym!"

"A trip to Kanto isn't complete without challenging the Rota Gym! Everyone should try it! Oh, by the way, Gym Leader Dylan uses a Water-type team, so I recommend using Grass-type Pokémon."

"Gym Leader Dylan is strong… By the way, the person above, are you okay?? (smile.jpg)"

"Hehe~ I told you all, this gym is pretty good."

Dylan was satisfied, looking at the positive reviews from the past few days.

(End of chapter)

---

# 24: Chapter 24: Selene

'For the next two days, the handsome Gym Leader Dylan will be out for training. Trainers who wish to challenge this gym, please come back in two days.

Friendly reminder, if you encounter the Gym Leader in the wild, there's a chance he might accept your challenge—Rota Gym Notice Board.'

"Gym Leader, is this alright?"

Today, the receptionist girl, with cute twin tails, smiled happily at Dylan after writing on the notice board.

"Very good. Your handwriting is beautiful."

Dylan praised her with satisfaction. Her writing was indeed very pretty.

"Hehe~ But Gym Leader, is this really okay? It seems like, apart from the Indigo League Tournament, no other gym takes holidays..."

The receptionist girl was happy to be praised but soon showed some worry on her delicate face.

"What if a challenger reports our gym for neglecting duties?"

"No worries. Gym leaders need rest too, and I'm not on holiday; I'm training."

Dylan spoke calmly. Normally, gym leaders, as the girl mentioned, only rest during the Indigo League Tournament.

This is mainly because when the Indigo League Tournament starts, no one challenges gyms for badges anymore since the competition has begun.

However, as a modern and good gym leader, Dylan believed he deserved some time off.

Working non-stop all year is not ideal; at least he should have weekends off to relax.

Moreover, he could use these weekends to experience the world's adventurous spirit, and he had a point to make—he was going out to train!

Who says gym leaders don't need to improve? Nowadays, the competition among gym leaders is intense. They face pressure from peers, challengers' public opinion, and inspections from the League.

Who knows when a pink-haired inspector might come down with their Latias and shut down your gym? (In the anime, the Kanto gym inspector is a Nurse Joy with a Latias. The Joy family is full of surprises.)

So, Dylan also needed to train hard to improve himself!

"It's a normal thing. If I don't train, how can I get stronger? If I don't get stronger, how can I better face challengers? Our gym's reputation is improving bit by bit."

"Many strong trainers might come to challenge, so I need to improve too."

Dylan spoke with a straight face, not feeling any shame.

Besides, Dylan knew from the original story that gym leaders often 'disappeared,' wasn't it a standard feature?

He was just blending in.

"Yes, I believe the Gym Leader can do it!" The receptionist girl nodded enthusiastically, her twin tails bouncing.

"Very good. Go home now, and remember to come to the gym in two days."

"Yay~"

Getting a two-day break, the receptionist girl was quite happy and ready to have some fun.

"By the way, Gym Leader, is the gym still hiring?"

"Hmm? Do you have someone to recommend?"

Dylan checked the time; Ilene hadn't arrived yet.

"Yes, I have a friend who's very interested in becoming a trainer, and she's a great person."

"Gym Leader, you're so amazing; could she come to the gym as an apprentice?"

The receptionist girl looked at Dylan expectantly. She wasn't interested in becoming a trainer herself.

Even though training was popular in this world, not everyone wanted to be a trainer, as it required a solid foundation.

The girl became the receptionist at Rota Gym because her family wasn't well off, and Dylan, knowing her situation, offered her the position out of kindness.

"Sure, since you recommended her, bring her in a couple of days."

Dylan pondered for a moment and then smiled.

"Great! Gym Leader, you can trust Selene. She's different from me; she's really amazing! She could recite type matchups easily as a child!"

"Selene?"

Dylan was surprised to hear the name. Selene?

"Look, Gym Leader, Selene is here! Selene~!"

The receptionist girl noticed something, her eyes lighting up, and she waved excitedly in that direction.

Dylan turned to see a slender black-haired girl with a pure, delicate face, wearing a yellow floral top and green shorts, her straight legs visible. She looked around fifteen or sixteen years old, with a pure and innocent aura.

Hearing the girl's voice, Selene quickened her pace, jogging over.

This...

Dylan's eyes flickered as he looked at Selene. Was this fate?

"Hey!." Selene smiled sweetly, clearly happy to see her childhood friend.

"Selene~ Long time no see! This is the Gym Leader, the leader of Rota Gym."

The receptionist girl hugged her friend tightly and quickly introduced Dylan.

"Gym Leader, this is Selene, my neighbor, whom I just mentioned."

"Hello, Gym Leader Dylan."

Selene politely bowed, her clear eyes filled with curiosity as she glanced at Dylan.

For this Rota Gym leader her friend spoke of, Selene was indeed very curious.

"Hello, no need to be so formal. Since you're a friend of her, just call me Dylan like she does."

Dylan's gentle voice made it easy to feel at ease.

"I heard that you want to become a trainer, right? How old are you now?"

"I'm fifteen, turning sixteen in five months."

Selene answered obediently.

"Then you can start traveling near the end of the year." Dylan nodded. No wonder she was still in Kanto.

"Would you like to join Rota Gym? I'm not a famous trainer, but I'll do my best to teach you."

Dylan smiled.

"Thank you, Gym Leader Dylan, I'd love to." Selene was pleasantly surprised. Becoming an apprentice at a gym is a rare opportunity for any aspiring trainer.

Her dream was to become a champion!

And her friend was desperately giving her encouraging looks.

"Yay~ Now Selene can keep me company every day."

The receptionist girl was thrilled, jumping up in excitement.

"Alright, come to the gym with her in two days."

"Okay~"

"Ilene is here. Gym Leader Dylan, we'll leave now."

"Go ahead."

Dylan watched the two girls leave, chatting happily, still feeling a bit emotional.

"What's up? Is she a friend?"

Ilene walked up to Dylan, noticing his unusual expression.

"Yes, a very talented trainer. What a coincidence~"

Dylan sighed at the coincidence.

He knew Selene was from Kanto, only moving to Alola with her mother at the start of the game.

But the original story never specified where in Kanto Selene was from. Turns out, it was from Rota.

"A very talented trainer?" Ilene was surprised. Those two words were quite significant, especially coming from Dylan.

"Yes, she's a prodigy~" Dylan glanced at Selene, who hadn't gone far yet.

And by 'prodigy,' Dylan meant it as a compliment.

This girl was as talented as Red and Blue!

Who would have thought?

This seemingly innocent girl not only single-handedly defeated the strongest evil organization, Team Rainbow Rocket, from a parallel world but also defeated the 'strongest legendary Pokémon,' Necrozma, three times, eventually subduing it.

"Let's go."

Dylan stopped thinking about it. Whether Selene in this real world had the same talent as in the game, she was now Rota Gym's first apprentice.

He would teach her well. Who knows, maybe a future trainer who shocks the world will come from Rota Gym?

(End of Chapter)

# 25: Chapter 25: Pokémon Inspection Bureau

Rota isn't small; it's more like a town with the scale of a city. However, because the area is quite remote and apart from the famous Cameran Palace, there aren't any other notable places nearby.

"Dylan, where are we going?"

Today, Ilene was wearing a casual long dress as usual. She seemed to love dresses, and Dylan had noticed that she could wear seven different styles in a week.

"Do you have any ideas?"

Dylan thought about it; he couldn't decide on anything himself. In fact, since coming to this world, this was the first time he was planning to go 'far away.'

"Um~ I don't know either." Ilene was a bit troubled, her eyebrows slightly furrowed in indecision.

As the current queen of Rota, Ilene usually stayed in the castle and rarely went out. This time, she finally had the chance to go out with Dylan.

"If we're talking about Kanto, the famous towns are all quite unique. If we're really stuck, why not check out the beach?"

Dylan suggested after some thought. This was also his first time planning to go 'far away.' If he were alone, he would have wanted to go to Lavender Town.

No other reason, but Lavender Town had many Ghost-type Pokémon nearby, and Kanto's most famous Pokémon cemetery, Pokémon Tower, was there.

Pokémon Tower is the largest cemetery in Kanto, comparable to Mt. Pyre in Hoenn, the Lost Tower in Sinnoh, and Celestial Tower in Unova.

Of course, according to later storylines, the Pokémon Tower would be rebuilt as Lavender Radio Tower, and the Pokémon buried there would be moved to the new cemetery, House of Memories.

Dylan wanted to go to Lavender Town to catch a Gastly line Pokémon; Gengar is quite powerful.

But since Ilene was accompanying him, and this was their first time going out to have fun together, Dylan didn't want to take her to a cemetery.

"Alright!"

Ilene smiled and agreed.

"Time is tight. Originally, we should have gone to Cinnabar Island, but we can't get tickets for the ferry until tomorrow. We won't make it in time."

Dylan checked his phone. Cinnabar Island is Kanto's most famous hot spring tourist island, very popular.

Today's tickets were sold out, so they could only book for tomorrow.

"How about we go to Vermilion City? They have a pretty good beach there too."

Ilene was a bit disappointed as she really wanted to experience Cinnabar Island's sun, beach, and hot springs with Dylan. But being the considerate queen, she suggested another location. They could always go to Cinnabar Island another time.

"Sure."

Dylan nodded. Compared to Cinnabar Island, Vermilion City was much closer.

After a brief discussion, they decided to call a cab to Vermilion City.

"Gym Leader Dylan."

At that moment, an officer Jenny in uniform walked over to them.

"Officer Jenny, is there something you need?"

Dylan discreetly observed the Jenny. Wearing Kanto's standard uniform, with light green hair and a beautiful, heroic face.

Hmm... couldn't tell.

Dylan gave up after a while. He'd seen several Jennys in Rota over the past few days but couldn't tell if they were the same person.

The Pokémon world is indeed fascinating, especially in terms of genetics. The name Jenny doesn't refer to an individual but a large police family in the Pokémon world.

The Jenny family is spread across various regions, much like the Joy family, and is a key pillar of the Pokémon League.

The most intriguing thing is the Jenny and Joy families' genetics!

Each family member looks almost identical, with only slight differences that are not easily noticeable to the average person.

It's quite amazing, isn't it? But considering there are people in the Pokémon world who marry Pokémon and even have offspring, it's not so surprising.

Thinking about human-Pokémon relationships, Dylan couldn't help but think of that handsome green-haired man in Unova.

He is a very special human. Dylan wondered if, in this world, he had already gained the Dragon God's approval.

Dylan pondered this and another crucial question he was curious about: Was that special human's father a Pokémon or his mother? And which Pokémon was it?

Dylan was really curious.

"Nothing important, Gym Leader Dylan, are you going out?"

Officer Jenny greeted Ilene, then smiled at Dylan.

Though each Jenny is a police officer full of justice, they are quite easy to get along with in daily life. And if you have trouble and ask for help, Jenny is usually willing to lend a hand if she's not busy.

"Yes, I plan to train in the wild for a couple of days to better handle challengers."

"It's great that Gym Leader Dylan has such dedication. It's a blessing for Rota."

Jenny was pleased. As Rota's Jenny, she had a strong sense of identity with the place.

Seeing the Rota Gym improve was only beneficial for Rota.

As for gym leaders going out for training for a few days, it was perfectly normal. No gym leader stays cooped up in their gym all the time like a hermit.

Gym trainers are trainers too, and they need to train.

"But Gym Leader Dylan, do keep track of time. Even if you're training, you can't stay away from the gym for too long."

"I understand. Thank you for the reminder." Dylan smiled.

"And one more thing, Gym Leader Dylan, if you encounter any strange challengers recently, please be cautious."

Jenny glanced around before whispering to Dylan.

"What do you mean?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"I can't say much; it's from higher up. But I believe Gym Leader Dylan can become an excellent gym trainer. I trust you'll handle it well."

Jenny spoke softly, her beautiful face showing goodwill.

That was all she could say. She had seen Dylan's transformation, from a newbie fresh out of Pokémon School inheriting the gym to someone who was seriously running Rota Gym.

"Thank you, I understand."

Dylan got the hint. Due to the bad reputation recently, the Pokémon League's Inspection Bureau must be sending someone for a secret inspection.

Pokémon inspectors regularly visit and investigate gyms, reporting to the Inspection Bureau, which then decides on any necessary actions.

Dylan took this seriously. Rota Gym was a new gym; if the inspector found it lacking, it would be a big problem.

Cerulean Gym could hold out until Misty returned from her Johto journey because it was Cerulean Gym.

Cerulean Gym is one of Kanto's official gyms and has contributed greatly to the Kanto Pokémon League, earning it a high tolerance from the League.

The decline in Cerulean Gym's reviews was due to a lack of new leaders, not because the inheritors weren't trying. The Kanto League knew Misty was training on her journey.

So, the League was willing to give Cerulean Gym a chance. However, a newly established Rota Gym didn't have such a background.

But it wasn't a big deal. Dylan believed he was now a fairly competent trainer and could handle an inspector.

"By the way, Gym Leader Dylan, the Inspection Bureau is setting up a group chat for Kanto gym leaders. This way, you can communicate and share experiences, helping each other improve."

Jenny was about to leave when she remembered this and informed Dylan.

"A gym leader group chat? Sounds good." Dylan was a bit surprised, but it seemed like a good idea.

"There are many excellent gym leaders in Kanto. You can learn a lot from experienced leaders like Gym Leader Giovanni from Viridian City. He's a very strong and skilled trainer, which will benefit you greatly."

"If you want to join the group chat, you can contact the Inspection Bureau."

"Thank you, Officer Jenny, I will."

Dylan smiled and nodded, though his expression was a bit odd.

Many excellent gym leader seniors in Kanto? Giovanni?

Hmm, no problem there. Giovanni's public persona as a gym leader is impeccable.

But if we're talking about the excellent gym leader seniors in Kanto...

Dylan's mind conjured images of several gym leaders.

In the games, it's fine; those gym leaders are responsible. But in the anime, their personalities are quirky, yet acceptable.

However, in the special manga version, it would be amusing.

(End of Chapter)

# 26: Chapter 26: Vermilion City

In the previous life, Pokémon, as the world's number one IP, had developed countless main and branch lines. The game series had already reached its ninth generation with "Scarlet/Violet," but undoubtedly, the most classic was the first generation.

The Kanto region is particularly unique, especially when it comes to its gym leaders.

The evil organization in Kanto is Team Rocket. Compared to the subsequent generations of evil organizations with various ideologies like expanding land and sea, reshaping the world, ideals vs. reality, or purging the world, Team Rocket's ideology is relatively 'pure': world domination!

Yet, without a doubt, Team Rocket is also the most straightforward 'evil' in people's minds among all the evil organizations. The anime, aimed at children, rarely reveals Team Rocket's true dark side, but both the games and the special manga provide descriptions.

In the special manga, Giovanni is depicted as a ruthless and cunning warlord who even dismembers a Pokémon in front of the protagonist.

Team Rocket's atrocities include robbing passerby Pokémon, hunting wild Pokémon, and conducting inhumane genetic experiments on Pokémon. The list of their crimes is extensive.

Yes, Giovanni, the leader of Team Rocket, is also the gym leader of Viridian Gym. Ironically, the Pokémon League is unaware of this fact.

Giovanni, the dark source of Kanto's underworld, is highly respected and revered in the public eye.

Dylan had briefly searched for information about Giovanni and found that, besides being a gym leader, Giovanni is also a businessman. Known as the 'Ground-Type Giovanni,' he is rumored to be the strongest gym leader in the Kanto region.

As an expert in Ground-type Pokémon, Giovanni wrote a widely circulated book, "The Secrets of the Earth," sharing his insights. This broad-mindedness earned him immense respect.

The Viridian Gym's positive rating has never been low over the years, despite Giovanni often being away 'training outside.' Few challengers obtain the Viridian Gym badge each year.

Yet, due to Giovanni's strength and reputation, the value of the Viridian Gym badge is high, and trainers take pride in earning the green badge from Viridian Gym.

However, no one would guess that Giovanni is the leader of Team Rocket, the biggest scourge of Kanto's underworld.

Of course, this doesn't concern Dylan right now. He only thought of this because of Officer Jenny's words.

If the gym leaders were like their special manga versions, Officer Jenny's words about 'excellent senior gym leaders' would be a complete joke.

"Speaking of which, Team Rocket will target Silph Co. later."

After bidding farewell to Officer Jenny, Dylan remembered this point.

Silph Co. is one of the world's top companies and the largest in Kanto by scale and output.

Silph Co. primarily manufactures products, excelling in the industry. The Poké Ball technology that trainers rely on today was pioneered by Silph Co.

One can imagine how massive a company holding the technology for Poké Balls would be in this world.

However, in this world, there is a clear divide between the trainer side and the life industry side!

Powerful trainers, especially Champion-level trainers, can be incredibly destructive, capable of destroying cities with their main Pokémon.

Though Silph Co. is wealthy—super wealthy, in fact—money doesn't equate to military power.

In the original story, Silph Co. was easily taken over by Team Rocket, who occupied its headquarters in Saffron City.

The president of Silph Co. was tied up and thrown in a corner like a dead dog. If the protagonist hadn't come to the rescue, Team Rocket would have drained Silph Co. dry, and all its assets would have been taken.

Ordinary people wouldn't risk angering the Pokémon League by breaking this rule, but Team Rocket is an evil organization.

The Pokémon League controls the balance between the trainer side and the life industry side. Anyone breaking this taboo would face severe retaliation from the Pokémon League.

But Team Rocket has no such scruples. Giovanni can do whatever he wants.

"I need to mention this to my dad; he's the largest shareholder of Silph Co."

Dylan thought. His father currently holds a 50% stake in Silph Co., making him effectively the owner.

So, Dylan is actually a super-rich heir.

Since that's the case, he can't let Team Rocket mess with his family business or let them take its assets.

"What's on your mind, Dylan?" Ilene asked curiously, seeing him deep in thought.

"Is it because of what Officer Jenny said? I believe in you, Dylan; you can definitely pass the Pokémon Inspection Bureau's test."

Ilene had also picked up on Officer Jenny's hint about the Pokémon Inspection Bureau. But she trusted Dylan would pass the test.

"Of course," Dylan smiled, snapping out of his thoughts.

"Let's head to Vermilion City."

"Alright~"

...

Vermilion City is located at the southern seaport, the largest coastal city in Kanto, and an international port city.

Vermilion Harbor serves multiple routes, and world-class luxury cruise ships often dock here.

The name 'Vermilion' comes from the orange-yellow color of the city at dusk.

At sunset, the entire Vermilion City is bathed in a layer of orange-red light, making the city look very soft and beautiful.

"The smell of the sea~"

After a few hours in a private car, Dylan and Ilene arrived in the city.

Ilene immediately felt the slightly salty sea breeze unique to the harbor city, which she found quite pleasant.

Which inland girl doesn't dream of seeing the sea?

"It's different from the game."

Dylan glanced around and commented briefly.

Over the past few days, he had completed the Vermilion Gym in the simulation. The real city, while retaining its characteristics, was different from the game.

He had already confirmed this in Pewter and Cerulean Cities.

"Huh? What game?" Ilene hadn't heard clearly.

"Nothing. Let's head to the hotel and then explore."

Dylan smiled and led Ilene to the hotel they had booked.

They went to the hotel, and while checking in, Dylan noticed someone entering and was surprised.

The man had long, steel-blue hair, long bangs covering one eye, wore gray clothes, a red cape, and had a face that was not particularly handsome but decent.

"Tsk~"

Dylan's eyes flickered as he looked at the blue-haired man.

This man was Tobias!

Perhaps many people wouldn't recognize this name because he had few appearances in the anime but was highly significant.

He appeared like a deus ex machina in the Lily of the Valley Conference, using his Darkrai to sweep through the competition.

In the semifinals, he used only Darkrai and Latios to crush Ash 6-2 and won the Lily of the Valley Conference!

Yes, the man known as the Legendary Pokémon Guy!

(End of Chapter)

# 27: Chapter 27: "Date"

It seemed that Tobias also noticed Dylan's gaze, so he turned his head to glance at Dylan.

Dylan smiled and nodded at him in a friendly manner. Tobias was stunned for a moment, then nodded back before heading toward the elevator.

In this world where travel is a popular trend, interactions between people are generally friendly. However, Tobias didn't seem very good at communicating with strangers.

Hmm, you could say he was a bit aloof.

"What's wrong, Dylan? Do you know that person?" Ilene asked curiously after seeing the interaction between Dylan and Tobias.

"No, I don't." Dylan shook his head.

"It's just that his outfit looked quite unique, so I was a bit interested."

Ilene: "..."

It seemed that Dylan was indeed socially awkward.

After getting their room keys from the front desk, the two of them decided to check out their rooms first.

However, to be honest, Dylan was still quite interested in Tobias.

In the anime, there were quite a few people who owned Legendary Pokémon, and Tobias was one of the classic ones, like Brandon.

But aside from the Lily of the Valley Conference, Tobias never appeared again, not even during the World Coronation Series in "Journeys."

This couldn't help but make people curious about Tobias's background.

To have captured Darkrai and Latios, either Tobias had a very powerful background, or he was incredibly lucky.

After all...

Tobias's strength was strong compared to ordinary people, but if you were to say he was truly powerful, that might not be the case.

How strong was Ash during the Sinnoh region? Obviously, not very strong, considering that his level was about the same as Paul's, and he was severely defeated by Flint, the Sinnoh Elite Four's Fire-type master.

In other words, Ash from the Sinnoh region definitely wasn't at the Elite Four level, not even close. Even if you used his old Pokémon, he still wouldn't reach that level. At most, he barely touched the threshold of the Elite Four, and that's only with his top-tier partners like Charizard and Sceptile.

But Ash still managed to defeat Tobias's Darkrai and Latios, which says something.

Moreover, being the winner of a regional tournament and being a Champion are two different things. Tobias won the Lily of the Valley Conference, which gave him the right to challenge the Elite Four and the regional Champion.

However, in the final challenge, it was Flint who challenged Cynthia, not Tobias.

In other words, Tobias either gave up the challenge, or he didn't even pass the Sinnoh Elite Four.

"He looks like he hasn't reached the age he was in the storyline. I wonder if he has Darkrai now?" Dylan was intrigued. The Tobias he just saw seemed younger than he was during the storyline, so it was unclear if he had already captured Darkrai, and why was he in Kanto?

But then again, the anime never explained where Tobias was from, so Dylan let it go.

After checking out their hotel rooms, the two of them, of course, stayed in separate rooms. After settling in for a while, Dylan and Ilene met up again and planned to explore Vermilion City.

"By the way, you two," as they reached the lobby, a hotel staff member approached them.

"Thank you for staying at our hotel. The S.S. Anne is currently docked at Vermilion Harbor and will be hosting a Trainer banquet tomorrow. If you're interested, you can check it out."

The hotel staff member smiled as they handed over two tickets.

"As a token of appreciation for our guests, these two S.S. Anne tickets are a gift from us."

"Is it the world-famous luxury cruise ship, the S.S. Anne?" Ilene covered her mouth in surprise, even she had heard of the S.S. Anne.

Because it is one of the most famous luxury cruise ships in the world.

"Yes." The staff member replied with a flawless service smile.

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Your hotel is really generous, huh?" Ilene was surprised. This hotel was indeed one of the top hotels in Vermilion City, but the tickets for the S.S. Anne were very expensive.

Giving out tickets just for booking a room? Wouldn't this hotel go bankrupt?

"That's not the case; this is just a temporary promotion."

The staff member's smile was a bit awkward. How could that be? The tickets were actually given to them for free.

Who knows what kind of idea the S.S. Anne had, organizing this Trainer banquet and suddenly giving out free tickets.

And this hotel just happened to get a few, which was a way to serve their customers.

"Thank you." Ilene was still very interested.

"You're welcome. Please leave a good review for our hotel."

"We will."

Dylan and Ilene walked out of the hotel.

"This really is a pleasant surprise! Dylan, let's check it out tomorrow."

Ilene carefully put the tickets in her bag and suggested to Dylan.

Although as the Queen of Rota, she wasn't lacking in money, and while the tickets for the S.S. Anne were precious, they weren't something she couldn't afford.

But since the tickets were free, why not use them?

"Sure." Dylan smiled and nodded.

"Let's go check out the pedestrian street."

"Sounds good!"

Even a queen couldn't resist the natural love for shopping.

Dylan hailed a taxi, and the two of them headed to the city's most famous pedestrian street.

As Kanto's largest port city, Vermilion City's daily foot traffic was enormous, and the pedestrian street was no exception.

Most of the people were from out of town.

The two of them shopped for about two hours, and Ilene bought a bunch of trinkets and clothes, which they had delivered to the hotel because it would be hard to walk around with all of it.

"This ice cream is really good."

After getting a bit tired, Dylan and Ilene bought two ice creams and ate them as they walked, and Ilene seemed to really enjoy it.

"Speaking of ice cream, I've heard that the ice cream from Castelia City in Unova is the most delicious." Dylan mused.

"I've heard that too. Dylan, take me there sometime."

Ilene's eyes lit up, looking at Dylan expectantly.

"Alright." Dylan agreed with a smile.

Ilene's big eyes curved into a sweet smile, a completely different look from her usual elegant and noble demeanor.

But just then—

Bang!

A young girl bumped into Dylan, almost stumbling.

"Sorry, sorry, I was texting my friend and didn't notice. Are you okay, mister?" The girl apologized quickly, looking flustered and uneasy.

Looking at his spilled ice cream, Dylan felt a bit helpless.

"It's fine. Are you alright? Did you get hurt?"

Dylan didn't blame the girl and gave her a quick once-over.

She was an ordinary girl, wearing a white top and a plaid skirt, with long brown hair and a slightly chubby face.

"I'm fine. I'm really sorry."

The girl rubbed her right arm, which hurt a bit from the collision, but it wasn't a big deal.

"Just be careful next time. This is a busy street, so you should watch where you're going," Ilene said gently to the girl.

Although the girl had bumped into Dylan, she had apologized, and it wasn't intentional.

"Thank you, sister. I'll remember that." The girl responded obediently.

"Brother, sister, can I go now? My friend is waiting for me."

The polite girl pursed her lips, looking a bit anxious but still seeking their permission to leave.

She was very well-mannered.

Ilene didn't make the decision for Dylan since he was the one who had been bumped into.

Dylan looked at the girl with interest.

"You can go."

"Thank you so much, mister! I'm really sorry about today."

The girl quickly thanked Dylan and grabbed her bag, ready to leave.

But just as she was about to walk past Dylan, a hand grabbed her arm.

"Mister?"

The girl's expression changed slightly, and she turned to look at Dylan in confusion.

"Mister, is there something else? Could you please let go of me? It hurts, sister?"

The girl looked worried and a bit scared, speaking softly. Seeing that Dylan didn't budge and just smiled at her, she turned her tearful eyes to Ilene.

Ilene was puzzled but trusted Dylan and didn't say anything.

The girl's eyes shifted again. This...

"Don't you have something you want to say?" Dylan's half-smile made the girl a bit anxious.

"Mister, it's not appropriate to grab a weak girl's arm in broad daylight, is it?"

Finally, the girl's tone changed, with a subtle hint of threat, clearly warning Dylan.

"I could accuse you of being a pervert!"

The girl lowered her voice, obviously not wanting to make a scene.

"Those aren't the words of a weak girl." Dylan shrugged.

"Besides, I could accuse you of stealing. My wallet is with you, isn't it?"

The girl's pupils dilated slightly.

"I'm not! I didn't! How can you falsely accuse an innocent girl!"

(End of the Chapter)

---

---

# 28: Chapter 28: I Prefer Excitement

The young girl appeared very angry, and her firm denial made Dylan feel a sense of admiration.

What a skilled actress~

"Dylan?"

Ilene, who was standing beside him, was shocked. Did Dylan just accuse this girl of stealing his wallet?

But Dylan wouldn't falsely accuse someone, right? Ilene looked at the girl with suspicion now filling her eyes.

"No! Sister, you have to believe me. I really didn't steal this big brother's wallet. How could I be wrongly accused like this…"

The girl looked aggrieved, tears welling up in her clear eyes, portraying the image of a helpless victim.

Ilene frowned slightly. She felt that the girl's expression seemed genuine, but Dylan wouldn't just target a girl like this without reason.

So, the girl must be trying to deceive her and gain her sympathy. But this act is just too convincing, impressive~

"Return my wallet, and we'll forget about this."

Dylan looked at the girl, who was still putting on an act, with a faint smile.

"You wouldn't want to attract the attention of Officer Jenny, would you?"

The girl's eyelid twitched.

"Deal!"

She immediately agreed. The pitiful look she had earlier vanished as she pulled Dylan's black wallet from her bag and handed it back to him.

"..." Ilene wanted to say something but held back.

"A young girl like you, fully capable, why resort to stealing? I'll give you a chance this time because you're still young."

Dylan opened the wallet to check that his cash and documents were still there before looking at the girl, speaking earnestly.

"?" A question mark seemed to appear over the girl's head.

"If we meet again, I will have to call Officer Jenny."

"Tch~ You act like you're much older, let's see what happens."

The girl laughed, amused by Dylan's words. She had roamed the streets for years and had never met someone this arrogant before!

She stuck her tongue out at Dylan and then quickly turned and disappeared into the crowd without looking back.

"Is it alright to let her go? She seems like a repeat offender." Ilene hesitated. Technically, they should have reported this to Officer Maple.

Though the girl seemed young and deserved a chance to reform, her actions suggested she wasn't new to this.

"This time, it was just a warning. If she chooses to continue, whatever consequences she faces are on her."

Dylan shook his head. He wasn't the type to back someone into a corner.

"You really are kind, Dylan." Ilene's eyes sparkled as she gazed at his profile.

"Not really? I don't think so."

Dylan denied it. He knew himself well enough.

Though in daily life he might come across as a warm and gentle person, Dylan knew better.

"Why do you say that?" Ilene was puzzled.

"I prefer excitement," Dylan said, truthfully.

Ilene was confused. What did he mean by excitement?

"If something interesting is about to happen, I would welcome it and might even stir the pot. In some sense, even if it has negative consequences, I might still push things along."

Dylan thought for a moment before elaborating.

If he had to give an example, he would love to see a clash between Team Rocket and the Kanto League, to watch Giovanni go head-to-head with Lance.

In fact, even if the Pokémon League asked him to help, he might just coast along because a full-scale battle between Team Rocket and the Kanto League would be fascinating.

"I see." Ilene still didn't fully understand, but as his childhood friend, she knew better than to pry further. She trusted Dylan's character.

"Dylan, I read online that there's a great BBQ place around here."

"Then let's give it a try, and afterward, we can go watch the river at night?"

"Sure~"

…

Elsewhere.

"Ugh! Seriously!"

The girl left the pedestrian street and walked into a deserted alley, pouting in frustration.

As she walked, the skin on her face and neck began to shift as if alive, until a light purple Ditto appeared on her shoulder.

Her appearance had changed from slightly chubby and ordinary to that of a pure and delicate beauty, a true young maiden.

"Strange, I didn't make any mistakes."

The girl tilted her head in confusion. From the targets' demeanor and expensive clothes, it was clear they were from wealthy families.

So targeting these rich kids was never a bad choice. Yet she hadn't expected to be caught red-handed.

"The Gym Leader of Rota?" The girl played with a business card she had slipped from the man's wallet.

That guy, who looked so young but spoke so condescendingly, turned out to be a Gym Leader?

After thinking for a moment, she decided to keep the card. A young Gym Leader who wasn't half-bad might come in handy someday.

"Who knows what Team Rocket is up to this time."

The girl didn't stop walking, her long legs moving gracefully like a dance.

She knew the truth: the sudden hosting of the Trainer Banquet on the S.S. Anne was because the ship was under Team Rocket's control.

"Heh~"

A bright smile appeared on the girl's face as she thought of something amusing.

By the time she walked out of the empty alley, she had changed her appearance once again, now sporting a freckled face as she blended into the crowd.

…

Time quickly passed to the next day.

"Wow~ This ship is huge!"

"Pika~Pika!"

At the dock, a young boy in a hat excitedly looked at the luxurious cruise ship before him, unable to contain his exclamation.

"Keep it down, you idiot!"

An orange-haired girl with a side ponytail covered her face, embarrassed.

The boy's voice had been so loud that people around them gave their group of three strange looks.

Even a man with long gray-blue hair and a cloak, who had just passed by them, glanced back curiously before boarding the ship.

"A world-class luxury cruise ship, it really is spectacular."

The tan-skinned, squinty-eyed man couldn't help but sigh.

"But I wonder why they suddenly decided to hold a Trainer Banquet. It's the first time, after all."

The orange-haired girl was still puzzled. Not only were they hosting a Trainer Banquet, but they were also giving away free tickets to trainers.

"Maybe it was just a whim of the captain."

"Could be."

Misty nodded in agreement but quickly realized the voice wasn't from her companion.

Turning her head, she saw a handsome couple before her.

"Are you also trainers attending the S.S. Anne's Feast? Nice to meet you."

Dylan smiled at the trio and their electric mouse companion, his gaze lingering on the boy in the hat.

The future strongest champion of the world, but for now, just a greenhorn.

(End of the chapter)

---

---

# 29: Chapter 29: The Future Legend!

"Yes! I'm Ash, and we're also Trainers. Of course, we have to give the Banquet a try!"

Ash's eyes lit up. Were the two people in front of him also Trainers?

"My name is Brock. May I ask… Ah! Ouch, ouch, ouch!"

"Could you tone it down, you fool! Do you not know how to speak appropriately for the occasion!?"

Brock had just started speaking when Misty swiftly and sharply pinched his ear, dragging him aside while scolding him angrily, as if frustrated by his behavior.

Brock was usually fine, except he seemed a bit too eager to find a girlfriend. Every time he saw a pretty girl, he would immediately try to strike up a conversation.

Usually, it wasn't a big deal, but they didn't know the relationship between the two people in front of them. What if they were a couple? Brock's words would be very inappropriate and could even be seen as a provocation.

"Emmm…"

Ilene gave the two of them a strange look. They seemed to have a really close relationship.

"Your companion is quite amusing."

Dylan couldn't help but smile as he spoke to Ash.

Indeed, the Misty and Brock from the anime were quite different from the ones in the games.

"Ahaha…"

Even the usually carefree Ash couldn't help but feel a bit embarrassed at this moment and could only laugh awkwardly.

"I'm Dylan, and this is my friend Ilene."

"Hello."

Dylan introduced himself to the three, and Ilene nodded with a graceful and elegant smile.

"I'm Ash from Pallet Town, and this is my partner, Pikachu."

"Pikachu~"

Ash flashed a big smile, and the electric mouse on his shoulder waved its little paw in greeting to Dylan and Ilene.

"What an adorable Pikachu."

Dylan couldn't resist reaching out to pet Pikachu's little head, finding the texture quite nice.

"Pika~!" Pikachu blinked its big eyes, a human-like smile appearing on its face, looking very cute.

"I'm Misty."

"My name is Brock, nice to meet you."

Misty and Brock, who had returned to normal, also introduced themselves.

The first meeting with Ash's trio was a pleasant surprise. They had originally thought they'd have to run into them again on the ship.

After a brief chat, they all entered the S.S. Anne.

The S.S. Anne was a world-class luxury cruise ship, with an interior that was extravagantly decorated, almost like the setting for a noble's ball.

In fact, it wasn't far from the truth. Dylan took a quick look around.

Though the theme was a Trainer's Banquet, many people present were successful individuals in suits and ties or noble ladies in evening gowns.

Of course, they might have dressed up on purpose.

"Ash, Ilene and I will take our leave now. Enjoy yourselves."

Dylan smiled as he spoke to Ash and his friends. The main reason he boarded the ship was because Ilene was interested in the Feast.

And regardless of the version, the S.S. Anne was always taken over by Team Rocket, so it was a chance to witness the event firsthand.

After bidding farewell to Ash and his friends, Dylan and Ilene began to stroll around the banquet area.

Though the venue was filled with socialites and noble ladies, it was still a Trainer's Feast, so there were quite a few Trainers on the ship.

The S.S. Anne had already set sail, and the atmosphere at the Feast was growing more lively. Some Trainers had even started battling.

"What do you think?"

Watching a battle between a Raticate and a Butterfree, Ilene, holding Dylan's arm, asked with a smile.

"The level is average, not as high as the previous challengers." Dylan shook his head. He and Ilene had already watched several battles.

But the Trainers who came to the Feast were pretty average, and even Ash at his current stage could probably beat these two on the field.

"Wow~ Impressive!"

"This guy's on a seven-win streak, right? He's been dominating every match."

"This Charizard is too strong!"

"Wanna place a bet? Let's see how many matches this kid can win in a row."

Scattered exclamations reached Dylan and Ilene's ears.

"Charizard?" Dylan became a bit more interested and walked in that direction with Ilene.

Charizard was the final evolution of the Kanto starter, Charmander. If it had already evolved to its final form, its strength wouldn't be weak.

As Dylan and Ilene approached, they finally saw the situation on the field.

A robust, powerful Charizard was standing on the ground, while its opponent was a Dodrio.

Unfortunately, even though Charizard didn't use its flying advantage, the Dodrio couldn't withstand Charizard's powerful Flamethrower and quickly lost the battle.

'Beep~ Charizard, Fire, Flying-type, Flame Pokémon

Ability: Blaze

Moves: Fire Spin, Scary Face, Flamethrower, Slash, Fire Fang, Dragon Rush (Inherited), Heat Wave, Air Slash...

Description: It can breathe intense flames that can melt rocks and often causes forest fires.'

What a strong Charizard! Ilene couldn't help but be amazed as she read the information from the Pokédex. This Charizard was definitely very powerful!

"Huh?"

However, when Ilene saw Charizard's Trainer, she was taken aback.

Dylan's eyes also flickered.

The Trainer was quite young, wearing a hat with clear red eyes beneath the brim, dressed in a red jacket, with dark blue jeans. His demeanor was pure and clean.

What surprised Ilene the most was how similar this person's facial features were to Ash's, whom they had just met.

But compared to Ash, this person didn't have such an outgoing and sunny disposition. His calm, emotionless expression made him seem a bit aloof.

Or maybe not aloof, but rather someone who wasn't good at socializing.

"Red...?"

Dylan gazed at the young man, unsure of what he was feeling at the moment.

The future legendary Trainer, Red!

The world sure worked in mysterious ways. Dylan never expected to meet Red for the first time here.

Meanwhile, on the field, Red shook his head, declining another challenger. After several battles, he had realized that the level of these Trainers was rather limited.

He recalled Charizard, ready to leave, but just then…

"Red!"

A joyful voice called out to Red.

Red turned around, and there stood the boy with the Pikachu.

"Ash~"

A smile appeared on Red's delicate face as he walked towards his beloved brother.

"Red, what are you doing here? You didn't even tell me!"

"I heard the S.S. Anne was hosting a Trainer's Banquet, so I came to check it out. But..."

Red didn't finish his sentence, but the disappointment in his voice was clear.

He had come here hoping to meet strong Trainers. Red loved battles, loved intense, powerful battles, and longed to give his all in a fight.

That was his belief, his pursuit.

But after several battles, he found that the Trainers here weren't much different from the random Youngsters he encountered on the road.

"What about you? How do you feel?"

Red's clear red eyes reflected the face of his brother, his usually calm voice softening.

As an older brother, Ash was his younger brother, and Red was truly very fond of him.

"It's a lot of fun! There are so many Trainers here. I just won three matches!" Ash proudly boasted about his victories to his brother.

"Well done, Ash."

Red's lips curved into a smile, praising his brother.

Every time he saw Ash's enthusiastic and joyful expression, Red couldn't help but feel happy as well.

Red understood that while his brother Ash might not be as naturally gifted as he was, Ash's love for Pokémon and his passion for battles weren't any less than his own.

Red also believed that Ash would one day become his rival! He always firmly believed that because... it was a promise he had made with Ash!

Ash might be trailing behind him now, but he would catch up!

S.S. Anne Captain's Room.

An old man was tied up, his hands and feet bound, with a white cloth stuffed in his mouth, lying pitifully in the corner.

This was the captain of the S.S. Anne, but now he was just a helpless old man.

And in his place sat a slender young man with short green hair, dressed in a white uniform.

Beside the young man, a fierce Houndoom lay on the ground, eyes closed as if dozing.

On the left chest of the young man's uniform was a large 'R'!

"What a beautiful and harmonious sight~"

The young man's fingers rhythmically tapped the desk, a faint smile on his lips as he leisurely watched the hall's monitors, exuding elegance and composure.

His name was Archer, one of Team Rocket's Four Executives!

While the Beast Trio expanded Team Rocket's influence in Johto and the three Admins operated in the shadows, in Kanto, the Four Executives were second only to Giovanni.

And Archer was particularly favored by Giovanni, excelling in both ability and strength.

"Archer, sir, the ship has reached the designated location! The target is also on board."

A Team Rocket subordinate in a black uniform entered the room and reported to Archer.

"Very good, then it's time to prepare a surprise for our guests."

A malicious grin spread across Archer's face, his pale complexion now completely shadowed, exuding a terrifying aura.

He had been lying low for some time, but now it was time for him to act.

When Team Rocket's mission succeeded and the S.S. Anne sank, Kanto would face a huge shock!

At that moment, Giovanni's power and influence would reach a new height!

"I'm looking forward to seeing their expressions when the S.S. Anne explodes~"

# 30: Chapter 30: You’ve Been Chosen

"I should've seen this coming. Ash, your relationship with Red..."

Brock gave a strange look at the two brothers, Red and Ash. In fact, Red had already come to challenge his Pewter Gym earlier.

However, apart from their appearance, the two were nothing alike! When Red came to challenge him, he was silent and efficient, crushing him with ease and taking the Gym Badge. He was all about ruthless efficiency.

He almost shattered Brock's fighting spirit.

Misty, on the other hand, curiously looked at Red. She hadn't encountered him before, but according to her sisters, several trainers from Pallet Town had repeatedly challenged them before Ash.

In the end, their Pokémon had even ended up in the hospital...

And Red was clearly one of the culprits. But Misty didn't think too much of it since she understood her sisters' skill levels.

"Thank you for looking after Ash."

Facing anyone other than Ash, Red seemed to revert to his usual 'cold' demeanor. But he still slightly tipped his hat, thanking Brock and Misty.

As his brother, Red knew Ash's personality well—carefree, absent-minded, and lacking in common sense. He was sure it was thanks to these two companions that Ash had been well taken care of.

"Hehe~ Let me tell you, Red is super awesome. Even Gary, that smug guy, respects Red a lot. When we were kids..."

Reuniting with his brother on the journey, Ash was obviously overjoyed.

But that joy didn't last long. Before Ash could finish his sentence, shouts suddenly erupted from a distance.

Then chaos began to spread, with various exclamations echoing continuously.

What's going on? The four of them looked over.

"Team Rocket!"

Misty, with her sharp eyesight, immediately noticed the Team Rocket members wearing black uniforms with a large "R" emblem on their chests and couldn't help but cry out in shock.

Moreover, more Team Rocket members were continuously pouring in through the hall entrance.

"Why is Team Rocket here?" Brock squinted his eyes and frowned immediately.

More importantly, why are there so many of them?

"Team Rocket again!" But Ash, being the fearless one, wasn't scared. Normally, the three of them were harassed endlessly by Team Rocket's duo and their talking Meowth, and now they were even showing up on the S.S. Anne.

Ash was furious. This was supposed to be his precious reunion time with his brother!

Red's red eyes calmly observed the Team Rocket members surrounding the hall. Those who were previously elegant and composed—ladies, gentlemen, and executives—were now panicking.

Team Rocket!

The absolute overlords of Kanto's underworld. They were known for stealing Pokémon, illegal trade, genetic experiments, and all sorts of evil deeds.

An organization strictly wanted by the Kanto League!

However, not many people had actually seen Team Rocket in person, but that didn't stop them from being afraid.

After all, the Team Rocket members in front of them had cold, ruthless expressions and looked extremely dangerous.

These Team Rocket members hadn't done anything too extreme yet—they simply surrounded all the passengers in the hall, though their attitudes were far from polite.

One overweight man in a suit moved a bit too slowly, showing reluctance, and a Team Rocket member gave him a hard shove, causing him to fall painfully to the ground.

But when he looked back, a fierce Raticate was glaring coldly at him. The man in the suit dared not say a word in protest.

"How can that guy do that!"

Ash's strong sense of justice flared up at the sight, and he was about to stand up and speak out.

Brock and Misty immediately tried to stop him.

"Calm down, Ash. Now isn't the time to act rashly."

A hand pressed down on Ash's shoulder, and a low voice spoke to him.

Ash was startled and turned to see it was Dylan and Ilene, whom they had met earlier.

"But…"

"I know you're upset, but we can't rush things now. Let's see what Team Rocket's up to first."

Dylan's eyes scanned the surrounding Team Rocket members. These were genuine members of an evil organization.

Even their demeanor and attitude were completely different from the comedic trio they usually encountered.

But where was that trio? Things had already taken a different turn from the anime.

"But they're really going too far…" Ash's face scrunched up in frustration.

"Dylan's right, you idiot. They have the numbers right now, so let's observe and wait for an opportunity."

Misty also spoke to Ash in a low voice. The hall was almost completely surrounded by Team Rocket members, and the sudden appearance of Team Rocket had given them the upper hand.

Starting a fight now wasn't a joke. And even if they did start, how many tourists would be willing to help? It was hard to say.

"Ash, calm down for now," Red said, also trying to soothe him, his red eyes calmly scanning the surroundings.

"Okay." Ash tightly pressed his lips together, suppressing his restless heart.

"This was a premeditated operation. We just don't know what Team Rocket's objective is."

Dylan lightly patted Ilene on the shoulder. She was quite nervous, as things had suddenly taken a turn for the worse with so many Team Rocket members showing up.

Red, Brock, and Misty understood the implication of Dylan's words.

There was no other explanation—how else could so many Team Rocket members have boarded the ship unnoticed? And this was just the hall; who knew how many more Team Rocket members were on the ship?

Could it be that Team Rocket had already taken control of the entire S.S. Anne?

What exactly did Team Rocket want?

"Of course, there's no signal on the phone." Dylan checked his phone and, as expected, the signal was blocked. Team Rocket wouldn't make such a rookie mistake.

In no time, Team Rocket had taken control of the entire hall, with Pokémon surrounding the passengers, watching them intently.

The atmosphere had dropped to freezing point. Some of the more mentally fragile passengers couldn't hold back their tears, their fear and anxiety reaching a peak.

"Good afternoon, esteemed guests~"

At that moment, a calm, elegant male voice came through the broadcast, filling the hall.

"I assume all our guests can hear my voice, correct?"

Indeed, the voice belonged to Archer, sitting in the captain's cabin.

"Team Rocket! What do you think you're doing? Do you realize the consequences of this?"

A middle-aged man dressed in designer clothes, unable to contain himself, looked up and shouted angrily. His rationality was being consumed by panic and rage.

Although he wasn't from a top-tier family or a leading conglomerate, he was still a well-known entrepreneur. But now?

He had been herded aside like trash by these Team Rocket members and was being casually mistreated. The humiliation and unease were driving him to the brink.

This was the world-famous S.S. Anne, and Team Rocket dared to hijack this ship and kidnap its passengers? This was a blatant challenge to the Kanto League's authority!

How dare they!

"Don't be angry, sir. We're currently in the middle of the vast ocean, far from the Kanto region. We have plenty of time."

Archer, hearing the middle-aged man's outburst through the monitors, smiled faintly, unperturbed.

He relished this kind of desperate outcry.

"First, allow me to introduce myself. I am Archer, an executive of Team Rocket. Today, we'll be spending some quality time together."

"I must sincerely inform everyone that you are honored to have been chosen by us."

Archer's voice was calm and composed, exuding an air of superiority, as if he was accustomed to such situations.

Hearing Archer's words, many of the passengers showed anger. They were being corralled and controlled like cattle, and he called it an honor to be chosen?

But in the end, the passengers dared not voice their anger.

"Please don't get upset. As long as everyone cooperates with us, Team Rocket isn't unreasonable. We'll ensure your safe return to land."

Archer's voice remained calm and polite, carrying a hint of a smile.

Dylan's eyes flickered. This journey on the S.S. Anne was turning out to be quite interesting.

Archer was one of Team Rocket's Four Commanders, a real high-ranking executive, and Giovanni's right-hand man.

For him to show up here, there must be something important at stake. But what could be on this ship that they wanted?

Dylan pondered. Could it be a rare Pokémon, a special individual, or something else?

(End of Chapter)

# 31: Chapter 31: Go Ahead and Explode!

"Now, the S.S. Anne is under the control of Team Rocket. We're currently in uncharted waters, and it would take at least two hours to reach the nearest land," Archer continued to 'reassure' the passengers with his calm voice.

He knew that some stubborn passengers would definitely try to find a way to call for help, but that didn't matter.

To ensure this operation went off without a hitch, the entire ship had been equipped with communication jammers. There was no way for phone calls or text messages to get through.

Even if someone managed to transmit a bit of location data through a special device, the S.S. Anne's route was completely random, and it would take time to locate them in the vast ocean.

Upon hearing Archer's words, some of the more delicate women began to sob, despair spreading among the passengers.

Aren't they just lambs to the slaughter for Team Rocket now?

"Then I believe everyone understands. Please obediently hand over your Pokémon and wait patiently for the S.S. Anne to dock."

Archer watched this scene through the monitor, a dark smile curling at the corners of his mouth.

"Are you kidding me?"

"If we give up our Pokémon, won't you be able to do whatever you want to us?"

"This is definitely a scam."

"Without our Pokémon, we have no way to protect ourselves."

The passengers weren't foolish. Their Pokémon were their last line of defense against Team Rocket.

Without their Pokémon, they'd be helpless, completely at Team Rocket's mercy.

"Everyone, please calm down~" Archer seemed completely unfazed, still speaking in a composed and unhurried tone.

"Don't get excited. I understand your feelings, truly. But please, take a look at this."

Archer gently soothed the passengers, gesturing for them to look ahead with a faint smile.

A large screen descended from the ceiling and lit up, displaying a scene to everyone.

"Hiss..."

The sound of people gasping filled the room, and many began to break out in cold sweat.

Even Brock and Misty had their pupils contract sharply. Ilene tightly grasped Dylan's hand.

Dylan gently patted Ilene's arm. He could feel that Ilene's body was trembling slightly—she was clearly scared.

And rightly so, because the scene on the screen was of the cargo hold of the S.S. Anne.

But the hold was filled with Voltorb and Electrode!

Who doesn't know these Pokémon? They're notorious for self-destructing at the slightest provocation, and now the cargo hold was crammed full of them, all in a deep sleep.

If one of them were to self-destruct, it would definitely trigger a chain reaction!

"How... how could they dare to do this!"

Misty's eyes widened in disbelief, fear making her voice pitch sharply.

"Team Rocket's madness truly lives up to its reputation," Dylan muttered in disbelief. This was exactly the kind of crazy stunt you'd expect from the largest criminal organization in Kanto, Team Rocket.

"This is..." Ash was stunned!

"They've probably used some special drug to keep the Electrode and Voltorb in a deep sleep. They wouldn't dare wake them up," Brock said, trying to remain calm, though he hadn't noticed his voice was trembling.

This wasn't a joke!

If that cargo hold full of Electrode and Voltorb were to explode, the entire massive S.S. Anne would be blown to smithereens, and everyone on board would be reduced to ashes.

Even Team Rocket wouldn't go that far, would they?

There's no need for such madness!

"But no one would dare take that risk. This is a game of life and death," Dylan nodded in agreement. Archer's tactic was indeed highly effective.

The psychological pressure on the passengers was now immense. There was no way they'd gamble against Team Rocket.

What if the worst did happen?

You only get one life, and once it's gone, everything's over.

At this moment, they finally understood why Team Rocket was considered an evil organization.

Red remained silent, his eyes fixed on the screen, his gaze flickering with a serious expression.

He was strong, but even he had limits. If those Voltorb and Electrode really did explode in a chain reaction, the devastation would be unimaginable!

"I believe everyone has calmed down. If you don't cooperate, hehe~"

Archer's laugh was low and grating.

"But you can all rest assured, as long as you cooperate, we'll definitely get you back safely."

In the captain's cabin, Archer's face twisted into a sneer, contrasting with the passengers in the hall. He was in a very good mood.

Giving them despair, then giving them hope—that's how fragile the human mind can be.

And this time, the hall fell silent.

"Well, let's begin."

As Archer's words fell, the Team Rocket members in the hall started moving forward with bags in hand, preparing to collect the passengers' Poké Balls.

Satisfied that everything in the hall was going according to plan, Archer nodded approvingly.

"Archer, we've located the target," a Team Rocket subordinate reported.

"Good. I hope Proton and that other guy succeed. This mission must not fail," Archer nodded. He wasn't interested in the passengers' Pokémon—ordinary ones weren't worth his time. This was just a distraction.

To ensure everything went smoothly, not only had he and another Executive, Proton, personally attended, but one of the three Admins had also been called in.

It's worth noting that the three Admins were the boss's secret weapons. They couldn't be used unless absolutely necessary, and revealing them would be a huge loss for Team Rocket.

"Tell Proton to wrap things up quickly."

Archer stood up and instructed the subordinate. Proton and the Admin's tasks were important, but he also had another crucial matter to attend to.

"Yes, sir!"

The Team Rocket subordinate responded and quickly left the captain's cabin.

"Hmm? What's going on?"

But just then, Archer noticed chaos unfolding in the hall through the monitor.

The passengers and Team Rocket members were fighting?

Weren't things under control? How did this happen in the time it took him to give a few instructions?

Were these passengers not afraid of death? How dare they resist?

Archer's expression darkened. Not even the threat of Voltorb and Electrode had cowed these people?

But Archer couldn't really wake up those Pokémon. While this mission was important, it wasn't worth risking their lives over.

Damn it!

Archer cursed under his breath, grabbing his Houndoom and quickly heading in a certain direction.

He had more important matters to handle. There was no time to deal with those damned passengers now. Once his own task was complete, he would make sure those passengers understood what it meant to face a Team Rocket Executive!

Meanwhile, in the hall...

"Wow, Ash really is something else."

Dylan glanced at Ash, who was now brimming with energy as he commanded Pikachu in a battle against a Muk.

Archer's plan wasn't wrong, but he had overlooked a special individual.

Someone who didn't care about threats and whose sense of justice and passion were infectious.

One rousing speech from Ash had woken the passengers up and sparked their rebellion. As for the threat of the Voltorb? It was just that—a threat.

If you have the guts, go ahead and explode!

(End of Chapter)

# 32: Chapter 32: Darkrai

At this moment, the S.S. Anne was in complete chaos. Various Pokémon were engaged in an all-out battle in the main hall, with Team Rocket clashing fiercely against the trainers.

"Evil Team Rocket! I'll defeat you right now!"

"Pikachu! Use Thunder Shock!"

"Charmander, Ember!"

Ash, full of energy, commanded his two Pokémon.

Electricity sparked from Pikachu as it shot out a stream of lightning, while Charmander puffed up its belly and spat out small fireballs.

"Heh, kid, do you think such weak attacks can make a difference?"

A Team Rocket member smirked disdainfully, his shadowy face filled with scorn.

The Thunder Shock and Ember hit the floating Weezing, but it only wobbled slightly, barely affected.

This Weezing was quite strong; this Team Rocket member was one of the top in the hall.

"Damn it!!" Ash gritted his teeth, feeling the pressure.

"Let me show you what real power is…"

The Team Rocket member sneered, about to boast, when a sudden, fierce flame surged at Weezing, engulfing it completely.

After an explosion, Weezing, charred black and emitting smoke, crashed to the ground with spinning eyes.

It… it was taken down in one hit?

The Team Rocket member was stunned!

"Red!" Ash looked overjoyed at Red, who wasn't far away. It was his Charizard's Flamethrower that had just taken down Weezing.

Red adjusted his cap, glancing at the still-dazed Team Rocket member.

Who does this guy think he is, trying to bully my little brother?

"D-Damn it!"

The Team Rocket member finally snapped back to reality, taking two steps back, gritting his teeth.

Damn, there's a big shot here!

On the other side:

"If this Charizard were in the game, it'd be at least level 45, maybe even Elite Four-level… And it's not even May yet. This guy's a total monster~" Dylan mused, glancing at Red and Ash while battling a Team Rocket member. Watching the powerful Charizard dominate the battlefield, knocking out opponents like they were nothing, he couldn't help but sigh.

The gap between people is even bigger than the gap between humans and dogs!

There were still six months until the Indigo League, yet Red was already at the level of an Elite Four trainer.

No doubt, by the end of the year, Red would reach Champion level.

But luckily, Dylan had his own advantages, including a trump card he carried with him.

Dylan shifted his gaze to the three Team Rocket members in front of him.

Noticing his look, the three Team Rocket members instinctively took a step back, their bodies tense and sweating.

"Aren't you going to attack?" Dylan asked curiously, puzzled by their ghostly expressions.

The three Team Rocket members, already pale, turned even whiter when they saw the two Pokémon in front of Dylan.

A terrifying Arbok, over four meters long, was hissing menacingly at them.

They knew this Arbok well.

The other was a sinister black, humanoid lizard with a long, wicked grin that seemed to stretch to its eyes—a truly terrifying sight!

It was a Salazzle, the evolved form of Salandit. After some time of training, Dylan's Pokémon had grown significantly stronger, with several evolving.

"W-We're out of Pokémon… They've all been defeated. Maybe you could go deal with someone else?" the tall Rocket member in the middle suggested nervously, trying to smile through his fear.

Whose idea was it to bring such a terrifying trainer onto the ship?!

All of their Pokémon had been taken out by Arbok and Salazzle. How could they possibly attack? Should they just throw themselves at him?

Ilene watched the three Rocket members with a strange look. So these were the notorious Team Rocket members...

"But if I'm not mistaken, each of you has only used three Pokémon," Dylan said, raising an eyebrow.

"Bro, we really don't have any left! We're not exactly proper trainers, so we didn't fill our teams," the chubby Rocket member on the left said with a pained expression.

"Is Team Rocket's treatment that bad? They don't even give you a full roster," Dylan remarked, genuinely feeling sorry for their lousy treatment.

"Yeah, yeah! Team Rocket sucks; it's not worth staying!"

The skinny Rocket member on the right nodded vigorously.

The other two Rocket members: "…"

"Alright then," Dylan said, amused, not pressing them any further.

"Remember to study hard, be a good citizen, and stay away from Team Rocket. They have no future; look to the League instead."

"Y-Yes, you're absolutely right!"

"We'll definitely be good people from now on!"

"If anyone dares bully a kid, I'll smack them myself!"

The tall, chubby, and skinny Rocket members all swore fervently.

"These guys are pretty funny," Dylan chuckled, watching them flee.

"Dylan, you're amazing~" Ilene said happily, feeling completely at ease with Dylan around.

"These were just grunts; we can't let our guard down yet," Dylan cautioned, recalling Arbok while keeping Salazzle out for protection in the chaotic situation.

Compared to the regular Rocket members, Archer was the real threat on this ship.

Dylan pondered for a moment. Although he had a powerful ace up his sleeve, it wasn't easy to get it to act.

In that case…

"Red, Ash," Dylan called, walking toward them. Red's Charizard was simply too strong, having defeated almost all of the Rocket members in the area.

"…"

Red turned his head, his red eyes glimmering as he gazed at Dylan.

It wasn't just Dylan who had been observing Red; Red had also been watching Dylan during the battle. Dylan was strong!

Brock and Misty also approached.

"Wow, Dylan, you're incredible! I saw you easily take down three Rocket members just now," Misty said in awe, especially eyeing Dylan's Arbok. It was incredibly well-developed, with a deep, chilling aura that was anything but ordinary.

"Not bad," Dylan replied humbly.

"Dylan is a Gym Leader at the Rota Gym, so of course, he's strong," Ilene added with a proud smile, her voice as gentle as ever but laced with undeniable pride.

"What? Dylan, you're a Gym Leader?" Ash was shocked, while Brock seemed to have recognized the name "Rota Gym."

"Is it the Rota Gym in Rota?" Brock asked with a strange look.

"Yes, Ash, Red, you should challenge it sometime," Dylan said with a smile.

"Sure! I'll definitely challenge it!" Ash said excitedly. Who would've thought his friend was a Gym Leader? He had to take on the challenge!

Red nodded as well, sensing Dylan's strength. A Gym like that was definitely worth challenging.

"You're always welcome, but for now, let's focus on the task at hand," Dylan said, glancing at the ongoing battle between passengers and Rocket members.

"The situation in the hall is under control, but Archer hasn't shown up yet, so we can't be careless. He's an Executive of Team Rocket and a very dangerous man."

"We need to find Archer. Red and I will search for him, while Ash, you stay here and continue helping the passengers. How does that sound?"

Dylan laid out his plan. Archer, one of the four Executives of Team Rocket, was likely not at Champion level but was probably on par with the Elite Four.

He and Red should be able to take him down. If they could defeat Archer, they could reclaim the S.S. Anne from Team Rocket.

"Okay," Red agreed. Beating all the regular Rocket members here wouldn't matter if Archer wasn't dealt with. He was the real target.

Meanwhile, on the other side of the S.S. Anne:

Boom!

Dark Pulse and Thunderbolt clashed violently, but the next moment, a ghostly purple figure with two pairs of wings glowing white dived swiftly, slamming into the nightmare-like Pokémon.

Darkrai grunted as it was knocked back.

Tobias' face darkened as he looked at the two figures before him.

A fierce-looking Raichu and a Crobat with flashing wings.

"An Executive like Proton, and an unknown Elite Four-level trainer… I'm honored," Tobias said solemnly.

"Given that you're Darkrai's trainer, we had to be careful. We're not really bad people," Proton said with a playful grin, twirling a Poké Ball and looking at Tobias and his Darkrai. His grin widened.

"If you hand over Darkrai, we won't harm you. How does that sound?"

Darkrai! The mythical Pokémon!

Team Rocket wanted it badly.

(End of Chapter)
