# 33: Chapter 33: Let Go of That Old Man!

"Are you really here for my Darkrai?"

Tobias frowned. He had already sent a signal to his grandfather through a special communication device, and someone was definitely on their way.

But first, he had to hold on until then.

"After all, Mr. Tobias, your Darkrai is quite tempting," Proton licked his lips.

"Having Darkrai in your hands, Mr. Tobias, is a bit of a waste. It could have a better Trainer, don't you think?"

Although Proton still used a respectful tone, the sarcasm in his words was evident.

What does that mean? Are you looking down on me?

"Impossible! Just the two of you think you can take my Darkrai?"

Even Tobias, who was usually calm, was now enraged. Trying to take his Darkrai and mocking him as an unworthy Trainer?

Who do you think you are!

"No need to waste words with him; just knock him down and complete the mission."

A deep voice sounded—another Rocket executive, this one tall and burly.

But it was clear that he was in disguise, wearing a large hat and sunglasses that covered most of his face, making it difficult to see his features clearly.

"If Mr. Tobias is unwilling, it's truly regrettable, so please excuse us."

Proton's face was filled with malicious intent.

Having a legendary Darkrai but only being at the executive level? Who wouldn't take it from him? It's his fault for being too weak.

Yes, Proton was very good at justifying his actions.

...

In the hall.

"But do you know where Archer is?" Misty asked.

The suggestion made sense. To defeat Team Rocket, they had to confront Archer.

"If there's no surprise, Archer should be in the Captain's cabin. He was there when he talked to us before."

"And we can also capture some Rocket grunts to interrogate them; they probably know."

Dylan spoke up. Archer's previous "speech" had definitely been from the Captain's cabin, but Dylan couldn't be sure if Archer was still there now.

But that wasn't a big problem. At worst, he could have Arbok scare those Rocket members—someone was bound to know.

"I know! I know where Archer is. I can take you there."

Suddenly, a lively female voice came from behind them.

Dylan and the others turned to see a girl in a black dress.

With a pure and delicate white face, her figure accentuated by the black dress, and long brown hair flowing down, she was a very pretty girl.

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"I just came from the hallway and happened to see Archer. I can take you to him."

The girl in the black dress smiled brightly, her voice full of energy, seemingly unaffected by the surrounding environment.

"Who are you?"

Ash was straightforward.

"Leah, Cerulean Leah, but you can call me Blue."

Leah glanced casually at Ash, then focused on Red and Dylan.

"Don't you trust me?"

You suddenly pop up and say you can take us to Archer—who would trust you so easily?

Dylan couldn't help but internally retort, though he knew Leah could be trusted.

"Even though I know you shouldn't trust someone of unknown origin, I really hate Team Rocket. Look at this."

Leah pulled out a key and waved it with a smile.

"This is the key to the ship's hold. I sneaked in and took it, along with the special potion to awaken the Voltorb. Archer is no longer there. If you go, you'll only find an empty room."

Hearing this, Brock and the others' eyes lit up. The key to the hold? That was great!

The hold was filled with Voltorb and Electrode, ticking time bombs. Now they could be much more at ease.

"Then we'll leave it to you." Dylan nodded.

Red glanced at Dylan, then nodded as well. There was no other choice now but to trust this girl named Leah.

"Ash, you stay here. Ilene is not a Trainer, so please protect her," Dylan requested of Ash and the group.

"Don't worry, Dylan! We won't let anything happen to Ilene!"

"Leave it to us, and you be careful too."

Ash and Misty responded immediately.

Even though they said that, Dylan still handed two Poké Balls to Ilene. He believed Ash would keep his word.

But honestly, Dylan had to admit that Ash's strength was still lacking, and Brock and Misty weren't much stronger either.

Red was momentarily surprised but then understood, handing Ash the Poké Balls for Venusaur and Blastoise as well. This would completely ensure Ash's safety.

Leah, meanwhile, watched with a smile, though a hint of envy and complex emotions briefly flickered in her eyes before she quickly concealed them.

"Always prioritize your safety, Ash. Don't get carried away. Misty, Brock, keep an eye on him."

Dylan couldn't help but add a final warning.

"Huh? What does that mean?" Ash was a bit annoyed. Was he really that untrustworthy?

"Don't worry, leave it to us!"

Misty patted her chest confidently, and Brock solemnly agreed.

"You be careful too."

After all, they were up against a high-ranking Team Rocket executive, so naturally, they were a bit worried.

With goodbyes exchanged, Dylan and Red followed Leah, running out of the hall.

There was little resistance along the way; most of the Rocket grunts had been sent to the hall, leaving the outside almost unguarded.

The few they encountered were easily defeated by Dylan's Arbok.

"What an impressive Arbok. By the way, are you really a Trainer from Rota Gym? The reputation of Rota Gym isn't like this."

Leah clicked her tongue in amazement at the strength of Dylan's Arbok and couldn't help but ask, intrigued by how strong it was.

Arbok wasn't typically a particularly strong Pokémon, certainly not on par with pseudo-legendaries, and not many Trainers focused on raising one.

As far as Leah knew, only Agatha, one of Kanto's Elite Four, used Arbok as a main Pokémon.

It was said that Agatha's Arbok was extraordinarily powerful.

"If you've seen the current reviews of the Gym, you'll find that there are some positive ones. My goal is to make Rota Gym famous in Kanto."

Dylan smiled.

"That may be, but those reviews seem a bit off."

Leah blinked, hinting at something.

Those so-called positive reviews didn't seem genuine to her.

Red listened, somewhat confused, but he wasn't good with words or social interactions, so he remained silent and aloof.

Dylan paused, giving Leah a thoughtful look. With communications on the ship being jammed, they couldn't browse the internet on their phones.

How did Leah know what those reviews were like?

Did she check before boarding? But what did Rota Gym have to do with her?

"We're here. This is the place."

The three of them finally arrived at a room. Leah signaled for silence and pointed to the door.

They didn't enter right away because Archer seemed to be talking to someone inside.

"Dr. Fuji, why resist?"

Archer's calm, composed voice could be heard.

"The organization has never mistreated you, yet you betrayed us. The boss has already said that if you return to Team Rocket, past grievances can be forgiven. What do you say?"

Fuji?

Dylan immediately recognized the name.

"Forget it. My greatest regret is listening to Team Rocket's lies and creating Mewtwo."

A calm yet weary voice replied.

"That's truly unfortunate, but I believe you'll change your mind, Dr. Fuji. Did you really think we didn't know where you went after betraying the organization?"

"You!"

"Dr. Fuji, you understand, don't you? Just hand over the research data on Mewtwo, and I can guarantee that those adorable children won't be harmed."

Archer's voice remained elegant and composed.

"You despicable scum!" Dr. Fuji was furious.

"Thank you for the compliment. But remember, Dr. Fuji, this situation is your doing, isn't it?" Archer chuckled softly.

"Well, Dr. Fuji, we've discussed everything. Please come with me now; I have other matters to attend to."

Archer was satisfied, seeing that Dr. Fuji seemed to have given up.

However...

Bang!

The door behind them was suddenly kicked open, and a clear, lively female voice rang out.

"Let go of that old man!"

(End of Chapter)

# 34: Chapter 34: Are You Ready to Go to Jail?

"Let go of that old man!"

As this sentence fell, the entire room went silent.

Professor Fuji, the old man, looked in astonishment at the two men and one woman who barged in. The boss of the S.S. Anne's "dungeon," Archer, was equally stunned, staring blankly at the three teenagers in front of him.

Dylan and Red exchanged strange looks, staring at Leah's back.

"She's really something~," Dylan couldn't help but whisper.

Red: (—)

Archer finally snapped out of it, his face twitching irregularly as he gave the three unexpected guests a sinister smile. His dark face now looked particularly eerie.

Alright, alright, alright!!! Not only did those useless subordinates fail to take care of the passengers, but they also let three little brats come here and show off in front of me?

Archer felt his temper rising!

"One, two, three—little ones, didn't your parents teach you not to interrupt your elders when they're discussing important matters?"

Archer slowly turned around, one hand behind his back, as a sinister smile crept across his lips.

The menacing Houndoom had already slowly walked to Archer's side, glaring fiercely at the three.

Dylan and Red exchanged glances and took two steps back.

"Sorry, but I don't have such an ugly elder like you, so don't try to act close."

"Today, the three of us are here to uphold justice! Archer, are you ready to go to jail?"

Leah, with her hands on her hips, declared righteously, causing Archer's face to twitch again.

What a sharp-tongued girl.

"Very well, disobedient children need to be properly taught."

Archer's expression darkened completely. Leah's words nearly made him laugh out of anger. Were kids today really this arrogant? He was one of Team Rocket's top executives, even arguably the leader among them, the boss's most trusted subordinate! And these little brats thought they could send him to jail?

"Dylan! Red!"

Seeing Archer's expression, Leah knew something was off, but it didn't matter. Though she admitted she definitely couldn't beat Archer, she had two strong allies! When she returned to the hall, she saw that Dylan and Red were both much stronger than her.

"You guys?"

But when she turned around, Leah was stunned.

At some point, Dylan and Red had already stepped out of the room and were standing in the hallway.

How could you do this!!

"Heh~ Houndoom."

The menacing Houndoom at Archer's side stepped forward, its mouth glowing with a fiery red light.

Leah reacted quickly, immediately turning around to run.

A Fire-type Flamethrower!

A surge of scorching flames shot out, directly aimed at Leah.

"Dylan! Your Arbok is just standing there doing nothing!"

Leah quickly shouted. She could already feel the heat waves behind her. Though her skin was protected by a Ditto, and she had a Wartortle's Poké Ball in her hand and other Poké Balls hidden in her chest, it didn't stop her from pretending to be a soft and innocent girl.

"Arbok, Throat Chop!"

Dylan couldn't help but chuckle as he ordered Arbok.

Arbok swiftly lashed out with its tail, coiled in dark energy, and fiercely struck at the incoming Flamethrower.

The Dark-type Throat Chop! Boom~!

With a loud bang, the flames were shattered by the powerful Throat Chop.

"Oh?" Archer was somewhat surprised by this.

"Phew~ That was close." Leah retreated to Dylan and Red's side, patting her chest in relief.

"You guys are so mean! I offended that guy on your behalf, and you just left me!"

Leah glared at the two. How could they do this!

"..." Red.

"The room's too small to fight in properly, and... you seemed to be having fun," Dylan said with a smirk as he looked at Leah.

"Hmph~!" Leah crossed her arms and huffed, but now wasn't the time to argue.

"I underestimated you three. You dared to show up in front of me, so you must have some skill."

Archer elegantly stepped out of the room, his gaze focusing on Dylan and the silent Red.

As for the pretty girl, he had already dismissed her as a show-off.

Where's your sharp tongue now? Why aren't you saying anything?

Dylan glanced at Leah, who pouted and turned her head away.

"Mr. Archer of Team Rocket, if we capture you and hand you over to Officer Jenny, the League's reward should be quite substantial."

With the sharp-tongued girl sulking, Dylan had to take the lead.

Saying a few words before fighting the boss is a classic ritual.

"Probably."

Archer seemed to agree, nodding slightly. His bounty with the League was indeed quite high.

"But only if you can do it. It's rare to encounter such powerful kids, so as a senior, I'll give you a chance."

Archer had found his rhythm, confidently walking to the other end of the hallway before turning around, with Houndoom facing Dylan and the others.

"Come, just defeat me."

Archer released another Poké Ball, and a Weezing floated beside Houndoom.

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Was Archer really that confident?

"Are there any other executives on this ship?" Dylan whispered to Leah.

"I don't know, but it doesn't matter. I already notified Officer Jenny in Vermilion City; they should be on their way."

Leah whispered back. You can't wander the world without a few tricks up your sleeve, right? The communication interference didn't stop her from sending out a distress signal.

Dylan thought for a moment.

"Red, I'll handle Houndoom. You take care of Weezing. Can you do that?"

Red nodded, releasing his Charizard.

"As expected, today's kids really are monsters~."

Archer scrutinized Charizard, feeling the heavy, scorching pressure, his expression shifting slightly.

This Charizard was definitely stronger than that Arbok! His intuition was spot on.

Archer glanced subtly at the room, his brow furrowing slightly. Professor Fuji was of critical importance; nothing could go wrong.

But with these three teenagers, it was impossible to take Professor Fuji away quietly. He had no choice but to battle them.

However, Archer was confident. Would a top executive of Team Rocket lose to a couple of kids who didn't look older than eighteen?

Impossible! Absolutely impossible! There's no way he would lose! "Old man, come over here."

Seeing that her two strong allies were ready for battle, Leah quickly waved to the old man inside the room.

"Oh, oh..." Dr. Fuji was a bit dazed, but he understood the situation and hurried to Leah's side, hiding behind Dylan and Red with her.

In the hallway, the tension grew even heavier.

"Dylan! Red! Go, go~ Take down that ugly guy!"

Leah, the mischievous girl, pulled out two support sticks from who-knows-where, cheering on the two in front.

Archer: "?"

Red: (^O^)

Dylan's eyelid twitched. Only Leah could pull something like this.

"Today, I'll show you what a real Trainer's battle looks like!"

Archer believed he hadn't been affected by the female clown, standing tall with one hand behind his back, a sinister smile on his lips as if he had everything under control.

One vs. two, the advantage is mine!

(End of Chapter)

# 35: Chapter 35: Mental Pollution

Meanwhile, on the other side.

Darkrai slammed heavily against the wall, its breath already extremely weak.

"Darkrai!"

Tobias was startled and looked at Proton and the mysterious trainer opposite him, his face turning pale.

With Darkrai down, he had no other fighting power left.

"Oh my, oh my~ It seems Mr. Tobias, you're done for."

Proton nonchalantly fiddled with his fingers, grinning as he looked at Tobias.

Tobias gritted his teeth.

"You two, as Elite-level trainers, actually teamed up against me! Shameless!"

Tobias's eyes were bloodshot. If it were a one-on-one, he wouldn't have lost!

But facing two trainers at once, unable to defend both ends, he really couldn't hold on!

Darkrai, as a Mythical Pokémon, was indeed powerful, and its physical strength surpassed that of ordinary Pokémon. But aside from Darkrai, his other main forces couldn't cope.

Damn it! Where's the League's rescue team? "Huh? Mr. Tobias, you must be out of your mind. We're from Team Rocket, you know~"

Proton laughed loudly, even clutching his stomach, making Tobias so angry he could barely contain himself.

This was blatant mockery!

"Stop wasting time with him, hurry up and recall Darkrai. I need to leave."

The burly man said irritably. He was here at Archer's request, and if not for his good relationship with Archer, he wouldn't have bothered coming.

After all, there was a risk of exposure.

"Don't rush, don't rush. We're almost done here. Don't you think this is fun?" Proton wasn't in a hurry, even stepping towards Tobias.

The burly man's eyebrows tightened under his sunglasses, seemingly understanding Proton's intentions. This guy was truly wicked.

However, the burly man didn't stop him; after all, they were colleagues.

Though as a Rocket Executive, he and Proton, one of the four Rocket Executives, belonged to different ranks, they were both subordinates of the boss.

"What are you trying to do?!"

Tobias instinctively stepped back as Proton approached, but then turned to see Proton's Crobat already hovering close by, far too close.

Tobias could even see Crobat's sharp fangs, which sent chills down his spine.

"Now~ Mr. Tobias, we've been quite reasonable, haven't we? I didn't even let my Pokémon ambush you."

Proton stopped in his tracks, looking at Tobias's pale face, grinning with a sigh.

"Tell you what, Mr. Tobias, I'll give you one more chance."

A malicious smile appeared on Proton's rather handsome face.

"The poison on Crobat's fangs is potent. If you don't get the antidote and timely treatment, Mr. Tobias, your life could be in danger."

"You!"

Tobias was both angry and scared. How dare this guy! "Shh~ Quiet, listen to me, okay? We're Team Rocket; we can do anything." Proton made a shushing gesture.

Tobias clenched his fists but remained silent, seething with humiliation.

"A Pokémon is a trainer's most important partner and companion. Even I believe that. Though I disregard other Pokémon, I care deeply about my own."

Proton leisurely began speaking.

"So, I want to know, dear Mr. Tobias, is Darkrai more important to you, or you're own life is more important?"

Proton smiled, his gleaming white teeth sending chills down one's spine.

Tobias's pupils shrank.

From the corner, Darkrai, too injured to move, turned its blue eyes towards Tobias.

"Think carefully before you answer. You only get one chance, and I can't guarantee what might happen if you choose wrongly."

Proton smiled maliciously, his tone still friendly, but the cold light in his eyes made the hair on one's neck stand up.

Tobias's face turned deathly pale. This...

The burly man glanced at Tobias, then at Proton, with a hint of disdain in his eyes.

Proton, despite his rather handsome appearance and polite, gentlemanly demeanor when interacting with others, was, in truth, the coldest and most ruthless among the four Rocket Executives.

In the burly man's view, Proton, with his pathological personality, could easily be ranked alongside Ariana.

Ariana was arrogant, hot-tempered, and extremely cruel, a detestable person.

And now, Proton was clearly tormenting Tobias.

Choose between your own life or Darkrai? This was a deadly dilemma. Did Tobias really think he wouldn't abandon Darkrai?

The burly man didn't think so. He had seen too many people like Tobias, psychologically fragile despite their arrogance.

And Proton wanted to see that result—Tobias's extreme humiliation and anger when he gave the answer.

Proton really was a psycho.

The burly man turned his head, not wanting to see any more. Though he didn't consider himself a good person, he definitely didn't want to be paired with Proton on missions in the future.

...

In another hallway.

"Arbok!"

Dylan called out to Arbok.

Arbok raised its body, its cold snake eyes staring down Houndoom and Weezing, exuding a commanding presence.

An invisible pressure spread, and the white glow surrounding Houndoom and Weezing dissipated.

This was Arbok's Intimidate ability, which could lower the opponent's attack by one stage! A very useful ability.

"Houndoom, Flamethrower."

Archer maintained his elegant demeanor.

Being Intimidated didn't matter, as Houndoom primarily relied on Special Attack in battle. Although Weezing's decreased Attack was a bit troublesome, it wasn't a big deal.

Houndoom's mouth glowed with a fiery red light, and it unleashed a powerful Flamethrower at Arbok and Charizard.

This Flamethrower was noticeably stronger than before.

"Flamethrower!" Red commanded in a deep voice.

Charizard roared, unleashing an equally intense stream of flames.

The two Flamethrowers clashed heavily in the center of the corridor, neither overpowering the other, eventually causing an explosion.

Smoke and dust rose up.

"Charizard!" Red's red eyes remained calm and bright as the battle continued.

Charizard flapped its wings, charging toward the opposite side under the cover of the smoke, with Arbok following closely behind.

"Weezing, Sludge Bomb!" Archer was clearly prepared.

Weezing let out a low growl, launching a series of sludge bombs.

A Poison-type move, Sludge Bomb.

"Flamethrower!"

Charizard opened its mouth again, breathing in and unleashing a burning column of fire. The flames clashed violently with the sludge, but this time, Charizard's fire clearly overpowered the Sludge Bomb. However, as Weezing detonated the sludge, another explosion rang out.

"Houndoom, Roar."

Getting close wasn't going to be easy. Archer smirked as he ordered Houndoom to roar, but just as Houndoom was about to use the move...

"Glare!"

A large cobra suddenly sprang out from behind Charizard, its abdomen markings glowing with an eerie light.

A Normal-type move, Glare! Archer was taken aback.

Houndoom instinctively looked at Arbok, and in its eyes, those eerie markings seemed to be spinning. Suddenly, its body froze completely.

Electric arcs flickered around Houndoom's body—it was paralyzed! This was the effect of Glare. Once hit, the target would be paralyzed.

And paralysis, causing stiffness and immobility, was fatal in battle.

Archer frowned; this was becoming troublesome. But what really irritated him...

The observing Leah's eyes lit up. That Glare was impressive.

At the same time, Leah wasn't idle, using her out-of-battle Mental Pollution move!

"Nice one, Dylan! Archer's really lame, huh? Getting caught like that."

Leah giggled, deliberately raising her voice so Archer could hear.

"Is that it? Is that it? No way, no way? Is that all Rocket Executive Archer can do?"

The sarcastic tone even made Dr. Fuji's eyes change, and Red couldn't help but glance back at Leah.

This girl...

Dylan: Amazing!

Red: (.)

And Archer, his face began twitching uncontrollably again.

This annoying woman's voice was really getting on his nerves!!! Archer glared coldly at the woman waving her cheer stick. Noticing his gaze, Leah didn't flinch at all and even made a face at Archer, sticking her tongue out!

Don't get mad, don't get mad! She's just a little clown! Archer kept comforting himself, but his mood was definitely getting worse. No one had ever dared to mock him like this!

But Archer forced himself to stay calm. That annoying woman was clearly using this underhanded tactic to mess with his mindset.

Ridiculous! As a Rocket Executive, how could he possibly...

"Dr. Fuji, don't you think it's funny? This guy was bragging before the battle, saying he was going to teach Dylan and Red how to fight, even calling himself a senior. But now, as soon as the battle started, his Houndoom got paralyzed."

"Next, he's probably going to say, 'Looks like I underestimated you; next time I won't be so careless,' right?"

Leah chattered away, vividly mimicking the scene.

Dr. Fuji was momentarily stunned by Leah's conversation, but after noticing her winking at him, he seemed to understand.

"Yes, it seems Archer isn't all that."

Dr. Fuji was still somewhat reserved.

But...

Archer felt his temperature rising. Dr. Fuji!!! This nasty, despicable, shameless, annoying clown of a woman!!!

I really want to kill you!

Well, Leah used the off-field move Mental Pollution, and it worked pretty well.

(End of this chapter)

# 36: Chapter 36: Yes, The Three of Us Are Strong!

Stay calm! You must stay calm in a battle!

Archer kept repeating this to himself, trying not to let the underhanded tactics get to him.

"Arbok, use Thunder Fang!"

At this moment, Dylan launched an attack.

Arbok opened its menacing jaws, electricity crackling around its sharp fangs, and lunged at Houndoom.

Electric-type Thunder Fang!

"Houndoom! Dodge it!"

"Weezing, use Thunderbolt!"

Archer reacted quickly, immediately giving orders to his Pokémon.

Houndoom had been paralyzed by Glare, but fortunately, the initial paralysis effect had worn off, and the paralysis would now trigger randomly.

However, due to the paralysis, Houndoom's movements had become noticeably sluggish.

But luckily, dodging a Thunder Fang was still possible.

Weezing, on the other hand, began to crackle with electricity, ready to unleash Thunderbolt on Arbok.

"Flamethrower!"

But just then, a scorching flame suddenly blasted towards Weezing, hitting it directly.

It was Charizard's Flamethrower!

Archer's face twisted in frustration as Weezing was sent flying by the Flamethrower. What was he doing just now? How could he have ignored Charizard?!

And at that moment.

"Oh dear~ You've been hit! At this rate, you'll lose soon, right? You'll lose to these two youngsters, won't you, old man? Isn't that right?"

"Ah, yes, yes."

A sarcastic, cheerful female voice chimed in, and Dr. Fuji glanced at Archer with a puzzled expression.

"!!!"

Archer glared at Leah, who was hiding behind Dylan and Red, with a cold stare.

This wretched woman! This clown!

"Arbok, use Stomp."

Dylan chuckled, noticing that Archer was losing his composure.

Arbok's expression grew more menacing as it lunged at Houndoom again, its powerful tail glowing with a brownish-yellow energy.

"Houndoom, use Flamethrower..."

"Dragon Claw!" Red's steady voice suddenly rang out.

Charizard's claws glowed with a sharp green light as it suddenly charged at Weezing and slashed.

Dragon-type Dragon Claw!

"Weezing! Use Thunderbolt!"

Charizard's Dragon Claw struck Weezing, and it immediately followed up with a Flamethrower. Although Weezing countered with Thunderbolt, it was clearly at a disadvantage.

Meanwhile, Houndoom was struck by Arbok's Stomp. As a Ground-type move, Stomp was super effective against Houndoom!

Especially since Thunder Fang had missed earlier, Stomp's power was now doubled, dealing a heavy blow to Houndoom!

And with Houndoom still paralyzed, how could it possibly fight back?

In the blink of an eye, the situation had turned dire. Archer was starting to panic.

Archer's pale, shadowed face was now filled with gloom. The strength of Dylan and Red had exceeded his expectations, but the most infuriating part was that female clown!

"Wow~ Archer-senpai, your Houndoom has fainted. Tsk tsk~ It doesn't seem to be much of a challenge, huh?"

"Arbok doesn't even have a scratch on it."

Leah clicked her tongue in amazement, continuing her psychological warfare.

As she looked at the backs of Dylan and Red in front of her, Leah's eyes narrowed in a smile.

Yes~ The three of us are strong!

Archer's face twitched as he recalled his fainted Houndoom, his icy gaze sweeping over Leah.

With Houndoom out of commission, the outcome of the battle was clear.

And Archer clearly had no intention of sending out any more Pokémon.

"Oh~ Archer-senpai, your gaze is so frightening. You wouldn't blame me just because you lost the battle, would you?"

Leah showed no fear, even pretending to be scared as she hugged herself, speaking pitifully.

Archer's lips twitched, and then he forced a stiff smile.

"Heh heh heh..."

A low, rumbling laugh seemed to squeeze out of his throat.

"Good! Very good! The younger generation these days is truly remarkable."

Archer ignored the female clown and instead focused his gaze on Dylan and Red, offering genuine praise.

Indeed, his earlier mistakes in the battle might have been slightly influenced by that female clown.

But he had to admit, these two young men were extremely talented.

"Are you interested in joining Team Rocket? I can personally recommend you to the boss. Believe me, Team Rocket will offer you generous rewards."

Archer recalled his badly injured Weezing and extended an earnest invitation to the two of them. These were real talents!

Red remained silent.

"Mr. Archer, are you joking with us? Given your current situation, you still want to recruit us?"

Dylan spoke with a slight smile. He was indeed interested in Team Rocket, but had no desire to join them.

A wise bird chooses the right tree to nest in, and with a wealthy benefactor in his corner, his future was bright.

Why join Team Rocket and become a public enemy?

There is only one true ruler in this world, and that is the Pokémon League! Plus, the position of League Champion is open to anyone skilled enough. If power and status were his goals, Dylan could simply challenge the Indigo Plateau.

"Of course, I won't deny that I've lost this battle."

Archer seemed to regain his composure, adopting his signature pose with his right hand behind his back.

"But this mission isn't a complete failure, and Team Rocket will ultimately succeed."

"Oh?"

Dylan and the others frowned slightly at his words, glancing at Dr. Fuji. Wasn't Dr. Fuji the target of Team Rocket's mission?

Dr. Fuji looked just as puzzled when he met Dylan and Red's gazes.

"Dr. Fuji is indeed important and was my primary objective. What a pity."

Archer gave Dr. Fuji a regretful look.

"Dr. Fuji, I sincerely urge you to return to the organization. Whether for yourself or for those children, you understand, don't you?"

Dr. Fuji hesitated, his expression conflicted.

"What nonsense! You're just a loser! Grandpa, don't listen to him. This guy is about to become a prisoner!"

Leah was clearly annoyed. What was Archer trying to pull?

Besides, she had already notified the Vermilion City police. Officer Jenny and her team were surely on their way by now.

Not a single member of Team Rocket on this ship would escape!

"Mr. Archer, do you really think you can leave here safely?"

Dylan held a Poké Ball in his hand. Archer's calm demeanor—did he have another trick up his sleeve?

"Heh heh heh... I will remember this loss. We will meet again, young men. And if you ever change your minds, Team Rocket will always welcome you."

Archer began to retreat quietly.

"Arbok!"

"Charizard!"

Dylan and Red immediately commanded their Pokémon, not willing to let Archer escape.

To Red, this Team Rocket executive was too dangerous.

To Dylan, it didn't really matter if Archer was captured or not. If captured, the League would offer some rewards.

If not, the ongoing conflict between Team Rocket and the Kanto League would only become more interesting. He could just sit back and enjoy the show.

So, either way was fine.

"Oh dear~ Archer, it seems you're in quite a predicament."

Suddenly, a mocking voice came from behind Archer.

A green-haired youth in a black uniform slowly approached, clicking his tongue as he looked at Archer, then glanced at Dylan and the others.

"Executive Proton."

Dylan raised an eyebrow. This person was indeed Proton, another one of Team Rocket's executives.

"Oh~ You know me, kid? Looks like I'm getting famous." Proton grinned.

"There are actually two executives here."

Leah's expression darkened. This was news to her, and it was definitely troublesome.

Archer and Proton, two Team Rocket executives.

"Not two, but three." Proton smiled and corrected her.

There were three Team Rocket executives on board?

Leah and Red both looked slightly alarmed.

"Is the mission complete?" Archer asked Proton softly.

"Of course, it wasn't difficult." Proton shrugged.

"Then let's retreat."

Archer nodded. At least one of their objectives had been achieved.

"The League members will probably arrive soon. It's not safe to stay any longer."

"Okay, I'll follow your lead. You're the commander." Proton spread his hands, then curiously looked at Dylan and the others.

He had received a secret communication from Archer, which indicated that Archer might have been in trouble.

Was it because of these three young people?

"Should we stop them? We could delay them until Officer Jenny arrives."

Leah whispered to Dylan and Red.

Red remained silent, his red eyes turning to Dylan for his opinion.

"Three executives... that's too dangerous. At least we've ensured the safety of the passengers."

Dylan pondered for a moment before suggesting the safest course of action.

Leah and Red, understanding the situation, did not argue.

Though letting Archer go was frustrating, the threat posed by three executives was too great.

None of Team Rocket's executives were pushovers. Even if they weren't Elite Four-level Trainers, they were close to that level of strength.

"Proton's target clearly wasn't Dr. Fuji, so what was the other objective?"

Dylan wondered, feeling as though he had missed something important.

(End of Chapter)

# 37: Chapter 37: Dylan! I Challenge You!

Archer and Proton retreated, and this operation was undoubtedly a failure.

After all, for Archer, Dr. Fuji was the main target, and it took them a lot of effort to locate him.

But there was no choice; at least another objective was achieved.

On Dylan's side, they first went to the captain's quarters to rescue the poor captain and then returned to the hall.

The hall was still in chaos, as the Rocket members had not yet received the retreat signal.

"Red, Dylan, you're back!"

As soon as Ash and the others saw Dylan's group return, they immediately rushed over.

Ilene walked up to Dylan, scanning him from head to toe with concern. She breathed a sigh of relief when she saw he was unharmed.

After all, Archer, a senior Rocket executive, was their opponent, and Ilene was naturally worried.

"How did it go? Did you beat Archer?"

Ash asked eagerly.

"Of course! With my help, we totally thrashed Archer," Leah said proudly, her hands on her hips.

Red and Dr. Fuji remained silent, while Dylan nodded in agreement.

"Wow! Amazing!"

"No surprise from you guys!"

"What about Archer? Didn't you catch him?"

Ash and his friends were thrilled.

"No, the Rockets had more than one senior executive on this mission, so we decided not to pursue," Dylan shook his head.

Even if they had three senior Rocket executives, it would have been difficult to delay them for long.

And after all, they were on the S.S. Anne in the middle of the ocean. If they pushed Archer too hard, the consequences would be unimaginable.

Not to mention the numerous Voltorbs and Electrodes in the hold. Although Dylan had a powerful weapon, it wasn't his Pokémon, and it was uncertain if it would help catch Archer and the others.

"What a pity," Ash said, disappointed.

"It was the right decision. The Rocket members are too reckless and could have done anything," Brock said rationally. Ensuring their own safety and that of the passengers was clearly more important than capturing Archer.

"The Rocket members are retreating," Misty noticed the Rockets in the hall starting to withdraw.

"Archer must have notified them," Dylan deduced at a glance.

"Don't let them escape! These scoundrels!" Ash shouted with righteous indignation. It was one thing not to catch the executives, but letting the regular Rocket members escape was out of the question.

"Yes, let's detain them. Officer Jenny should be arriving soon," Dylan said.

The rest of the task was easy.

Without Archer and Proton, the remaining Rocket members were leaderless. With Dylan and Red joining the fight, they quickly defeated all the Rockets and detained them.

Half an hour later, the Vermilion City police arrived and took over the S.S. Anne, placing the Rocket members in handcuffs. Officer Jenny approached Dylan and the others.

"Thank you for your help. The Rockets even dared to hijack the S.S. Anne. Without your intervention, the consequences would have been unimaginable," Officer Jenny said with a serious expression, having understood the entire situation.

"It's what we should do, though it's a pity we couldn't capture Archer and Proton," Red remained cool, while Leah stayed out of the limelight. Dylan stepped forward to speak.

"You've done an excellent job. We will definitely catch those two dangerous individuals," Officer Jenny said, shaking her head. Archer and Proton, being Rocket executives, were not easy to capture. She was more interested in the trainers who could defeat Archer.

"Would you mind giving us your names? I'll apply for a reward and make sure your heroic deeds are known," Officer Jenny asked with a smile, respecting Dylan and the others' decisions. Not everyone liked publicity, especially since they had antagonized Team Rocket.

Dylan's eyes brightened. This was a great opportunity to advertise the Rota Gym!

"I'm Dylan, a gym leader from Rota."

"..."

"Hehe, I don't need any recognition. Give my share to Dylan and Red," Leah smiled and withdrew again, not wanting to attract attention. If her organization noticed her, it would be troublesome.

Dylan noticed the complex emotions in Leah's eyes. This cheerful, lively girl hid many secrets. But Dylan said nothing, as there was nothing he could do for her against that organization.

"Alright, I understand," Officer Jenny nodded, looking curiously at Dylan.

"Are you also a gym leader?"

"Yes, I was just accompanying a friend on this cruise. I didn't expect this to happen."

"I'm sure you're an excellent gym leader," Officer Jenny smiled. She hadn't heard of the Rota Gym, but any gym leader who could defeat Archer would surely become famous.

"I'll do my best," Dylan replied.

Officer Jenny didn't stay long as there were many Rocket members to process. Fortunately, the passengers were mostly unharmed, except for one person...

On the deck, Tobias stood lost, staring at the endless horizon, his expression bleak.

"Mr. Tobias, rest assured, we will capture the Rockets and retrieve your Pokémon," an officer said sternly, but Tobias was unresponsive.

He felt his life was over, his mind returning to the moment when Proton forced him to abandon Darkrai...

Tobias couldn't imagine Darkrai's look when he made that choice.

His eyes welled up, and he pulled out his phone, dialing a number. With the interference device off, the S.S. Anne was returning to Vermilion City.

The call connected quickly.

"Tobias, are you okay?" An elderly, kind voice answered.

"Grandpa..."

Hearing his grandfather's voice, Tobias couldn't hold back his tears.

"Grandpa, the Darkrai you gave me... It was taken by Team Rocket."

Tobias felt both wronged and sad.

"..." On the other end, the old man was too shocked to speak.

Darkrai was taken by Team Rocket? How did this happen? Even with his status, a legendary Pokémon was incredibly precious. Darkrai followed Tobias because of the old man's connection to its ancestor.

"Grandpa..."

"Don't be sad, Tobias. Those Rockets..." The old man's voice was tired. Team Rocket was a pest in Kanto.

"I have information on a Latios. I can give you its location, but Tobias, this is the last time I'm helping you," the old man said sternly. Finding legendary Pokémon was extremely difficult, and helping his grandson contradicted his principles.

But Tobias was his grandson, so he would help one last time. If Tobias still couldn't succeed, then so be it.

"Thank you, Grandpa," Tobias said with teary eyes, understanding his grandfather's disappointment. Damn Team Rocket! I will have my revenge!

...

In Vermilion City, at the Pokémon Center.

"What a trip! But it's time for me to go," Leah said, retrieving her healed Pokémon from Nurse Joy and waving goodbye to Dylan and Red.

"Bye-bye," Dylan smiled.

"Goodbye."

Red, rarely speaking, responded. Except for family and a certain rival, he was usually silent.

Leah smiled and prepared to leave.

Red turned his gaze to Dylan, his eyes burning.

"?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Dylan, I want to challenge you!" Red said eagerly.

Hearing this, Leah, who was about to leave, stopped and turned around, her eyes sparkling.

Wow! Are these two powerhouses about to fight?!

# 38: Chapter 38: Well Said, Come Work at My Gym

Hearing Red's words, Leah really couldn't move her feet.

Her two rivals were about to battle! How could she leave?

Dylan noticed Ilene looking at Red in surprise. This young man sure had guts.

They had just returned to Vermilion City, and he was already challenging him?

"Betting on a badge?"

Dylan wasn't too surprised and smiled in response.

The S.S. Anne incident didn't really affect him, and he believed Red felt the same way.

Red's eyes were blazing as he nodded.

Seeing this, Dylan couldn't help but chuckle softly.

"As a qualified gym leader, I won't refuse any challengers. So, Red."

"I accept your challenge as the leader of the Rota Gym."

Dylan looked directly into the young man's scarlet eyes, speaking clearly.

Yes, as a gym leader, he had the duty to accept any challenger's challenge, even if they weren't in the Rota Gym at the moment.

Red's lips curled slightly, though very faintly.

"Alright!! I'll be the referee for your battle! Where should we fight?"

Leah cheered, seemingly more excited than the two combatants.

"Let's borrow the battle field from Nurse Joy."

Dylan suggested. In fact, every Pokémon Center had a training field in the backyard.

It was available for trainers who came to the Pokémon Center to use for special training.

"Great! I'll go ask!"

Leah eagerly ran to the front desk.

"Miss Joy~ My two friends want to use the backyard battle field. Is that okay?"

Leah asked sweetly, her big, watery eyes giving a hint of a playful tone.

"Of course, would you like me to arrange a referee for you?"

Nurse Joy, with her pink hair and gentle smile, replied softly.

She was actually very busy at the moment because the S.S. Anne was docked, and many injured Pokémon were brought to the Pokémon Center.

"No need, thank you, Miss Joy."

"You're welcome~"

Leah thanked Nurse Joy and then turned to Dylan and Red, giving them an OK gesture.

At the Pokémon Center's backyard.

Dylan and the others arrived there. As a rest and treatment station set up by the Pokémon League for travelers, Pokémon Centers were established in almost every region, city, town, and village.

The facilities were comprehensive, and so were the battle fields. Some scattered trainers were also training there.

Choosing an unused field, Dylan and Red stood at opposite ends.

Ilene, along with Ash's group, who had heard the news, stood on the sidelines as spectators.

"Go for it, Red! Pikachu, cheer too!"

"Pika~ Chu!"

Ash cheered enthusiastically for his dear brother and even forced his electric mouse to cheer.

Pikachu rolled its eyes before reluctantly shouting.

"Brock, who do you think will win?" Misty asked curiously about the battle between the two.

"Definitely Red!"

"Family members of the combatants, please be quiet!"

Misty glared at Ash, making him shrink his neck. Misty's temper still left a shadow.

"A battle between Dylan and Red..."

Brock looked at the two standing apart, squinting his eyes in contemplation.

"Honestly, if it were before, I would have believed Red would win. You might not understand; I've never seen such an outrageous rookie trainer."

Brock gazed at the boy with pure red eyes under his hat, feeling both impressed and emotional.

"He has incredibly striking qualities. Red's absolute sincerity in battle is something I've never seen before. He's a born trainer!"

Brock's evaluation of Red was astonishingly high, but since he had battled Red, he felt qualified to say such things.

Only someone who truly and deeply loved Pokémon, loved battling, and enjoyed it immensely would possess such a quality—the passion for battle!

Brock remembered vividly that before Red, another trainer from Pallet Town had also crushed him and taken his badge.

That trainer's Pokémon were so strong they didn't seem like a rookie's. The trainer's innate arrogance and pride were unforgettable.

But that trainer was completely different from Red.

This sincerity for battle, combined with Red's monstrous talent, would undoubtedly create a terrifying trainer. Brock believed this firmly.

"The Rota Gym... I hate to say it, but I remember its reputation wasn't very good, right, Ilene?" Brock looked at Ilene, trying to be polite.

"Yes, a little over a month ago, the Rota Gym was rated one of the weakest gyms in Kanto."

Ilene spoke gently.

So it was true.

Brock nodded. He remembered because Rota wasn't far from Pewter City, so he had seen the reviews of the Rota Gym on the Kanto League's official website.

"Really? But Dylan is very strong."

Ash was puzzled.

"The Rota Gym was inherited by Dylan from his father. Back then, Dylan had just graduated from Pokémon School."

Ilene shook her head.

"At that time, Dylan only had a newly received Sprigatito and a few friendly Pokémon from his backyard."

"During that period, no matter who the challenger was, Dylan couldn't win, and the gym's reputation declined."

"No matter how hard Dylan tried, he couldn't win."

As Dylan's companion, Ilene witnessed that period firsthand. Her tone turned somber.

"..."

Ash and Brock were silent, while Misty's expression grew complex, seemingly empathizing deeply.

Why did she leave Cerulean Gym in a fit of anger? It was because she wanted to travel and train herself.

Her sisters had no talent as trainers. Though their water ballet performances kept Cerulean Gym popular, it was a gym, not a dance hall!

Challengers mocked the badge as being practically given away, which was a huge insult to a gym meant to train challengers.

So Misty traveled to become stronger and then return to revive Cerulean Gym.

"But half a month ago, it was as if Dylan had an epiphany. He became... very strong."

"He told me."

Ilene looked at Dylan in the arena with gentle eyes.

"He will make the Rota Gym the strongest gym in Kanto!"

Ilene's lips curved into a happy smile.

Ash blinked, Brock seemed thoughtful, and Misty was a bit stunned.

What could they say? They were all quite moved.

In the arena.

"Now, let's start the battle for the Rota badge! The challenger is Red from Pallet Town."

"The battle will be 4 vs. 4. If all four of the challenger's Pokémon are unable to battle, the challenge is over."

"Begin!"

Leah raised a small flag, announcing the battle rules clearly, which Dylan had just told her.

"Well said. If you want, you can apply to be a referee at my gym."

Dylan clapped his hands, not sparing any praise for Leah.

"Humph~ Of course. How much would you pay me to work for you?"

Leah crossed her arms with a proud expression.

"I'm a super-rich second generation."

Dylan couldn't help but laugh. Sorry, money was just a number to him.

"Really?" Leah doubted his words.

"My dad is the largest shareholder of Silph Co. What do you think?" Dylan casually flaunted his wealth.

"No way!" Leah was stunned and instinctively blurted out sweet words.

Wait, wasn't Dylan just a gym leader? Was he really a rich second generation?

"What?!" Misty and Brock were also surprised, while Ash didn't quite understand the significance.

Red remained silent, his sharp eyes fixed on Leah, wondering why she hadn't left the field yet.

"We can talk later; it's battle time now."

Seeing Leah's eyes gleaming as if she was about to pounce on him, Dylan laughed.

Leah blinked and then wisely left the battlefield.

Red: (ˇ︶ˇ)

"Sorry to keep you waiting, Red."

Dylan smiled at the red-eyed boy opposite him.

"Before the battle, let me formally introduce myself."

"My name is Dylan, the leader of the Rota Gym."

Dylan slowly adjusted his collar, his movements casual yet inexplicably free-spirited.

"My current specialty is the Poison type. Have you ever battled against Poison-type Pokémon?"

"Poison will leave you helpless and miserable!"

Dylan's lips curled into a smile as he took out a minimized Pokéball, twirling it deftly.

"It's not just about absolute power. Experience the fear and horror of Poison-type Pokémon!"

Facing Dylan's declaration, Red's eyes grew even brighter.

"Bring it on!"

Red slightly lowered the brim of his hat, the shadow covering his blazing red eyes, gripping his Pokéball tightly.

He couldn't wait!

Trainer Red! Here to challenge!

---

---

---

# 39: Chapter 39: The Privileges of the Wealthy

"Whoa~ Are they about to have a formal battle?"

"Using the scoreboard too, it seems so."

"Wonder what their skill levels are like?"

"Wow! It's the two big shots from the ship!!"

As Dylan and Red prepared to battle, a small crowd began to gather around them, whispering amongst themselves.

"Brock, could you do me a favor?"

"Miss Ilene, please, go ahead."

Brock was initially surprised but quickly regained his composure.

Even though he knew the extraordinary relationship between Ilene and Dylan, helping a beautiful sister without hesitation was his creed!

Of course, if it was something unethical, Brock would refuse.

"Could you record this battle for me? I'm not very good with this."

Ilene handed the drone camera to Brock apologetically.

"Let me see..."

Brock squinted his eyes, took the drone and controller, fiddled with it a bit, and successfully launched it.

Brock then found the best angle and stopped the drone, nodding in satisfaction as he watched the feed.

"As expected of you, Brock." Ilene sighed in relief.

"Miss Ilene, why did you suddenly want to record this battle?" Misty asked curiously.

"Because Red is a very strong trainer. This battle will surely be exciting and memorable."

Ilene smiled in response.

Of course, she had a bit of a personal agenda. If she could upload this battle online, it would attract a lot of attention, which could also bring traffic to the Rota Gym.

Of course, Ilene would have to ask for Dylan and Red's permission after the battle.

...

On the battlefield.

"Arbok! Prepare for battle!"

Dylan threw his first Poké Ball backward.

A large, robust purple cobra landed in front of Dylan, slowly raising its upper body and flicking its tongue, glaring at Red with a cold gaze.

Dylan didn't start with his Glimmet, even though its Corrosion ability was very suitable as a lead Pokémon.

However, it would struggle against Red's Blastoise, not only being countered but also because Corrosion only activated when hit by physical attacks.

Additionally, Blastoise knew Rapid Spin, a move that could clear the field.

"It's that Arbok again."

Ash took out his Pokédex, even though he had already recorded Arbok.

'Beep~ Arbok, Poison-type, Cobra Pokémon.

Ability: Intimidate.

Moves: Sludge Bomb, Glare, Toxic Spikes, Venom Drench, Fire Fang, Ice Fang, Thunder Fang, Earthquake, Gunk Shot...

Description: The pattern on its belly looks like a terrifying face. Weak opponents will flee at the sight of this pattern.'

"Wow! So many moves, it looks super strong!"

Misty widened her eyes. This Arbok was extraordinary!

"Arbok is one of Dylan's favorite main Pokémon, so he has taught it many moves."

Ilene smiled as she explained.

"This is how the rich teach their Pokémon..." Leah marveled. While there were many ways for Pokémon to learn moves, such as self-realization, special training, or being taught by others, the simplest way was using TMs. However, TMs were not cheap due to their convenience.

For Dylan, this was not a problem. Silph Co. was a top-tier global enterprise, and his dad was the largest shareholder, earning astronomical dividends each year.

Therefore, Dylan's allowance was in the millions, and he could have as many TMs as he wanted.

Moreover, according to Itto a few days ago, Silph Co. was making the most powerful Poké Ball yet, which would be rare due to the special materials required.

Yes, Itto had ordered one for Dylan.

"Blastoise!"

Red also quickly threw his first Poké Ball.

In a flash of white light, a large, bipedal blue turtle Pokémon landed heavily in front of Red, glaring fiercely at Arbok.

It was Blastoise, the final evolution of Squirtle.

'Beep~ Blastoise, Water-type, Turtle Pokémon.

Ability: Torrent.

Moves: Hydro Pump, Iron Defense, Shell Smash, Aqua Tail, Rain Dance, Protect, Rapid Spin, Flash Cannon, Water Spout (Egg move)...

Description: Its heavy body can cause opponents to faint when pinned down. It hides in its shell when in danger.'

"Awesome! As expected of Red's Blastoise!"

Seeing the imposing Blastoise, Ash was very excited. So cool!

He also had a Squirtle and believed it would evolve like this in the future!

"A pure Water-type Blastoise versus a pure Poison-type Arbok. Neither has a type advantage." Brock held his chin while adjusting the drone's camera position.

"Starting with Blastoise, huh?"

Dylan squinted slightly, his eyes flickering with thought. He was formulating a strategy.

Blastoise's Rapid Spin could clear Corrosion, so this Blastoise had to be dealt with quickly. The sooner it was defeated, the better.

However, judging by its oppressive presence, this Blastoise was undoubtedly strong.

"Arbok!"

Arbok let out a raspy hiss, releasing an invisible pressure that enveloped the area.

Blastoise's white glow faded, its Intimidate ability lowering Blastoise's physical attack by one stage.

Arbok slowly swayed its tail, its cold eyes full of anticipation.

"Arbok, use Earthquake!"

Dylan made the first move.

Arbok's strong tail swung up and then slammed down heavily on the ground.

A Ground-type Earthquake!

Rumbling sounds erupted as the battlefield shook violently, sending a tangible shockwave forward.

Under the intense tremors, Blastoise's body wobbled uncontrollably. If not for its strong base, it might have toppled.

"Blastoise, Rapid Spin." Red's voice was calm and steady.

Blastoise quickly retracted its limbs and head into its shell, spinning rapidly into the air.

Earthquake could only affect grounded opponents, and Blastoise, floating with Rapid Spin, naturally avoided the attack.

"Hydro Pump!"

Red wasn't just trying to dodge.

From the spinning shell, a powerful water jet shot out, heading straight for Arbok.

A Water-type Hydro Pump.

"Sucker Punch!"

Arbok's eyes flashed with dark energy, and its large body turned into a purple serpent shadow, darting toward Blastoise.

A Dark-type priority move, Sucker Punch! This was Arbok's Egg move.

With Sucker Punch's speed boost, Arbok dodged Hydro Pump effortlessly.

It swiftly reached beneath Blastoise, coiling its body before leaping up to strike.

"Can't dodge this."

Red instantly understood. In Sucker Punch's state, Arbok's leaping speed was too fast. Even with Rapid Spin, Blastoise couldn't outpace it.

However, Red remained calm. Blastoise's shell was extremely hard!

Arbok's Sucker Punch landed on the rapidly spinning Blastoise, its tail shrouded in dark light striking hard against Blastoise's shell.

Sucker Punch!

Thud~!

A dull thud echoed, resonating with the hardness of Blastoise's shell!

Arbok felt as if its tail had struck steel, even causing it pain.

The spinning Blastoise was knocked back and stopped, falling to the ground.

"Hydro Pump!" Red commanded instantly.

The falling Blastoise's cannons glowed with water energy.

Two massive water jets, one in front and one behind, shot toward the also descending Arbok.

"Sludge Bomb!"

Arbok opened its mouth, launching dark purple sludge bombs, a Poison-type Sludge Bomb.

Boom, boom, boom!

Sludge Bombs clashed with the Hydro Pumps, exploding loudly, but soon one Hydro Pump broke through, continuing towards Arbok.

In terms of special attacks, Arbok couldn't compete with Blastoise.

The Hydro Pump struck Arbok directly, causing it to cry out in pain and crash to the ground.

Blastoise, though also falling, suffered little damage. Its hard shell blocked the attack, taking minimal harm.

Undoubtedly, Red held the advantage in the first round.

"As expected of you, Red."

Dylan smiled, unfazed by this temporary setback. The first round was merely a probe, and it was clear that Arbok's raw power wasn't enough against Blastoise.

Beating Red in a straight battle was very tough, as his strength and Pokémon levels were high.

Yes, Dylan needed to use some other tactics.

# 40: Chapter 40: Agony

"Impressive~ Dylan is truly impressive~!"

Ash's eyes sparkled when he saw Blastoise's Hydro Pump finally hit Arbok.

"Indeed, Blastoise's shell defense is excellent, making Arbok's fierce attack nearly ineffective," Brock praised. Blastoise's ability to counterattack was due to its shell blocking Arbok's assault.

"But this Arbok is also very strong. I've never seen an Arbok raised this well," Misty chimed in, blinking in amazement.

She knew that Agatha, one of the Elite Four, also had an Arbok, reportedly quite special, but Misty had never seen it herself. Undoubtedly, this Dylan Arbok's was the strongest she'd ever seen.

The recent ambush was incredibly fast! If it were her Starmie, it might not have reacted in time and would have been hit.

"But Red will definitely win!" Ash had absolute confidence in his brother.

"Without breaking through Blastoise's shell, Arbok indeed has little chance," Brock mused, knowing Arbok wasn't adept at special attacks. The earlier clash of Sludge Bomb and Hydro Pump proved that Arbok's physical attacks couldn't penetrate Blastoise's shell and thus couldn't cause much damage.

On the surface, Arbok seemed to have no chance, which was reasonable.

"You can't be sure. Dylan has said he's actually better at using poison," Leah's beautiful eyes sparkled slightly, and she spoke with a smile.

Yes, that's a significant variable!

Brock looked at Dylan curiously. Poison-type trainers were very rare, and he was eager to see how Dylan would perform.

...

On the battlefield.

"Blastoise! Water Spout!"

Red slightly lowered his cap, his red eyes under the brim fixed on Arbok, and he initiated the attack.

Blastoise opened its mouth, blue light flashing, and then sprayed a massive amount of water, surging towards Arbok like a flood.

The exaggerated water volume shocked many onlookers.

Water-type Water Spout! It's one of Blastoise's inherited moves, known for its powerful special attack because its power depends on the user's stamina.

In its current good state, Blastoise's Water Spout was exceptionally powerful, nearly covering half the battlefield and leaving Arbok almost no room to dodge.

"Dig," Dylan chuckled.

Arbok's cold eyes stared at Blastoise behind the water, then suddenly burrowed underground.

Ground-type Dig! As the name suggests, it allows the user to dig underground.

The water from Water Spout flooded Arbok's previous position, with much of it flowing into the dug hole, but it clearly didn't affect Arbok.

On the battlefield's surface, only Blastoise was visible.

"Rapid Spin!"

Red's eyes flashed. The best way to counter Dig was with Earthquake, but Blastoise hadn't learned that move yet.

So it was simple.

Blastoise retracted its limbs and spun rapidly, rising into the air.

"Arbok!"

A four-meter-long purple cobra suddenly sprang from the ground beneath Blastoise, lunging at it.

Arbok was also enveloped in a faint yellowish-brown glow, the energy of Dig!

Bang!

Dig hit Blastoise hard, making it shudder and halting its Rapid Spin, knocking it back slightly.

But like the previous ambush, Dig struck the shell, causing limited damage.

"Hydro Pump! Hmm?" Red immediately commanded Blastoise to counterattack, but then frowned.

This time, Arbok didn't pause after the attack but lunged at the nearby Blastoise again, its long body wrapping around Blastoise's shell several times.

This was Normal-type Wrap, binding the opponent tightly.

Blastoise growled in discomfort, glaring at Arbok's head, which returned a cold stare.

"..." Red was silent.

In this situation, Hydro Pump couldn't hit Arbok since Blastoise used it either from its mouth or its back cannons.

But there were many ways to break Wrap.

"Withdraw."

Red calmly ordered, using Water-type Withdraw.

Blastoise's limbs and head retracted into its shell, and a white light glowed, boosting its defense by one level.

"Arbok! Thunder Fang!"

Dylan gestured to Arbok.

Arbok understood instantly, baring fangs crackling with electricity, though it was merely a feint.

Arbok thrust its head into Blastoise's shell, pretending to use Thunder Fang but actually spraying dark purple venom inside, covering Blastoise's retracted head.

Blastoise was bewildered. Wasn't it supposed to be Thunder Fang?

"Gyro Ball."

Red frowned, sensing something was wrong, but he couldn't let Arbok cling to Blastoise.

Despite the uncomfortable venom on its face, Blastoise began to spin rapidly.

Steel-type Gyro Ball!

A metallic sheen appeared on Blastoise, and the increasingly fast spinning made Arbok unable to hold on, flinging it to the ground.

Blastoise immediately turned to attack Arbok, but then a dark purple glow appeared on the Gyro Ball.

Red noticed this, his eyes narrowing. What was this?!

Gyro Ball's speed decreased, and though it hit Arbok, the impact wasn't strong due to the reduced speed.

Gyro Ball's power depends on the speed difference, and since Blastoise and Arbok had similar speeds, the move wasn't very powerful.

More importantly, Blastoise's unusual condition drew attention.

Blastoise extended its limbs and stood, eyes shut tightly, its face twisted in pain, clearly suffering from severe poisoning.

Its entire body flickered with a dark purple glow, with poison spots visible on its limbs and head.

Such obvious symptoms needed no explanation... it was poisoned!

And it was badly poisoned!

"..." Red frowned.

"What is this?"

"Poison! But when did it happen?"

"But Dylan didn't command Arbok to use Toxic!"

"Impressive~!"

Ash and the others were stunned and bewildered.

"Thunder Fang was a ruse. Toxic was the real attack."

Red understood now. If it were truly Thunder Fang, biting Blastoise would have spread the electricity.

The initial poisoning passed quickly, and Blastoise glared at Arbok, the poison's pain fresh in its memory.

If not for its strong will, it might not have been able to continue Gyro Ball.

But being poisoned made the battle difficult. Red's eyes flashed.

"Arbok, Earthquake!" Dylan smiled slightly.

Arbok's tail glowed yellow, smashing into the ground upon landing.

Boom!

Ground-type Earthquake!

The ground trembled, and the shockwaves spread out.

"Rapid Spin." Red quickly strategized.

Everything was going well, but the poison changed it. Toxic wasn't ordinary; its effects worsened over time.

Blastoise spun into the air, dodging the Earthquake.

Though it had just been poisoned, the damage wasn't too high yet, so Blastoise could still fight.

"Water Gun!"

Blastoise, mid-air, stretched out its head and limbs, and blue energy glowed in its mouth.

The next moment, a massive flood-like water stream gushed out, far more impressive than Surf.

"Ambush!"

Dylan opted for Ambush instead of Dig.

Arbok's eyes flashed with black light as it swiftly moved forward, fast as lightning.

Dark-type Ambush!

Arbok reached Blastoise's previous position quickly, just as Water Gun began to fall.

In the next moment, Arbok leapt, shooting towards the falling Blastoise.

"Blastoise! Withdraw!"

Red's voice was steady.

Withdraw, then counter with Hydro Pump, should work. But...

The worry became reality. Blastoise stopped Water Gun and tried to retract into its shell, but halfway through, the dark purple venom flared up again, causing excruciating pain and halting its movement.

The poison struck!

Bang!

Arbok's Ambush hit Blastoise's head, making it grunt and fall heavily to the ground.

"Earthquake!"

Arbok's tail glowed yellow again, smashing the ground upon landing.

Earthquake!

Boom!

The severe tremor, combined with the poison's erosion, caused Blastoise immense pain.

Hissing sounds from onlookers and Misty indicated their empathy.

Just watching, one could sense Blastoise's agony!

The series of attacks was terrifying!

(End of Chapter)

# 41: Chapter 41: First Point

"This…"

Ash's mouth hung open in shock. The situation had changed so dramatically in such a short time.

His brother Red had the advantage just moments ago, but now, Blastoise was in serious trouble.

Even though he was still young, Ash understood that the series of blows Blastoise had taken had likely caused severe damage.

"Dylan timed the activation of Toxic perfectly!" Brock exclaimed in amazement.

"When Toxic activates, the excruciating pain causes the Pokémon to stiffen up, and he just happened to catch the timing with Sucker Punch."

He knew the activation time of Toxic all too well. As expected of a Poison-type trainer.

"Blastoise doesn't stand a chance anymore," Leah agreed with Brock. The three-hit combo of Toxic activation, Sucker Punch, and Earthquake was simply overwhelming.

Coupled with the previous damage, it was a wonder Blastoise hadn't already fainted, thanks to its excellent defenses.

But now, Blastoise was barely holding on, in a very poor state.

"Return, Blastoise," Red recalled his heavily injured Blastoise, his gaze full of regret as he looked at the Pokéball.

If it weren't for being poisoned, this situation wouldn't have happened.

But getting poisoned was his responsibility as a trainer.

"You're really strong!" Red's eyes burned with intensity as he returned Blastoise to its Pokéball.

This was the first time—apart from his childhood rival Blue—that he'd been at such a disadvantage in a battle against another trainer his age.

Even though it was the poisoning that caused him to lose, Red didn't see it as an issue. Using poison was just another battle strategy.

Winning with poison was still a victory.

"You're strong too," Dylan responded with a smile. Recognition from a rival, especially one who was evenly matched, was always satisfying.

"Come on, let's continue this battle!" Dylan's spirits were high. The fight had only just begun.

"Pikachu!" Red threw out his second Pokéball.

"Pika~!"

A flash of white light, and a nimble electric mouse landed gracefully in front of Red.

Red didn't choose to use Venusaur. Although Venusaur was immune to poison, it would still struggle against Arbok.

Besides, preventing poison wasn't too difficult.

And Red had great confidence in his Pikachu, which was extremely quick and agile. Landing a Toxic on Pikachu? That was wishful thinking!

"Red's Pikachu!" Ash's eyes lit up. Pikachu was his favorite!

"Pika~Chu!" Ash's own electric mouse also sparkled its big eyes as it tilted its head to watch Red's Pikachu on the battlefield.

'Beep~ Pikachu, Electric-type, Mouse Pokémon.

Ability: Static

Moves: Thunder, Light Screen, Thunderbolt, Discharge, Iron Tail, Agility, Electro Ball, Feint, Volt Tackle…

Description: When several Pikachu gather, their combined electricity can cause lightning to strike.'

Red's Pikachu knows so many moves!

"Oh wow~ Both brothers raised a Pikachu, huh," Leah remarked, her curiosity piqued as she glanced between Red and Ash's Pikachus. No wonder they were siblings!

Actually, Pikachu wasn't a rare Pokémon, and normally, its strength wasn't very high.

Ash was still a beginner trainer, so it was understandable, but Leah was curious why a seasoned trainer like Red would also raise a Pikachu.

She didn't believe Red couldn't find a Thunder Stone to evolve Pikachu.

"Because Pikachu is strong," Ash replied as if it were the most obvious thing in the world. He genuinely believed it.

"Hmm…" Leah smiled but said nothing.

On the battlefield.

"Red's Pikachu…" Dylan looked at the adorable electric mouse with a strange expression.

He remembered the time when he was still young, playing the Gold/Silver games. After finally defeating the Johto Elite Four to become the Champion, he ventured to Mt. Silver.

And there, he encountered a silent, hat-wearing trainer. As soon as the battle started, out popped a Pikachu, and when he checked its level—81!

It was ridiculously high! At that point, his team was only around level 60, and suddenly, an 81-level Pikachu appeared out of nowhere—how could he possibly win?

The game designers sure knew how to surprise.

His reminiscing was cut short as he refocused on the battle with Red.

"As a Gym Leader, not being able to switch Pokémon is a bit of a pain."

Dylan pondered how to handle the battle. Arbok wasn't the best matchup against Pikachu because of Pikachu's small, agile frame.

If it were anyone else's Pikachu, it might be different, but Red and Ash's Pikachus were special.

Even in Gym battles in the games, leaders could freely switch their Pokémon, but in reality, the League rules stated that a Gym Leader couldn't switch Pokémon during a challenger's battle.

Unless both parties agreed beforehand. Leah hadn't mentioned this rule, but as a competent Gym Leader, Dylan imposed it on himself anyway.

But it wasn't a big deal.

First, he needed to gauge this electric mouse's strength.

"Pikachu, Electro Ball!" Red initiated the attack this time.

Pikachu's energetic cry was adorable as it jumped high, forming a ball of electrical energy on its lightning-shaped tail.

An Electric-type Electro Ball! The move's power increased the greater the speed difference between the two Pokémon.

"Sucker Punch!"

Arbok's eyes flashed darkly, and its long body swiftly darted towards Pikachu, easily dodging the incoming Electro Ball with the added speed from Sucker Punch.

Sucker Punch was a priority move. Usually, such moves had lower power, but Sucker Punch was an exception because it required the opponent to attack first, so it had decent power as well.

With Sucker Punch, Arbok quickly closed the distance and lunged at Pikachu.

"Iron Tail!"

Pikachu's tail shimmered with a metallic sheen before it swung it hard at the approaching Arbok.

Bang!

Iron Tail collided heavily with Sucker Punch, and both Pokémon were knocked back from the impact.

A strong physical attack.

Dylan raised an eyebrow, clearly impressed with how well Red had trained his Pikachu. Its level must be quite high.

If only this were a simulation world, Dylan would love to see exactly what level this Pikachu was.

"Thunderbolt!"

As Pikachu was pushed back, its cheeks sparked with electricity.

An Electric-type Thunderbolt!

"Sludge Bomb!" Dylan commanded Arbok to counter.

Arbok opened its mouth, a purple glow forming inside.

But before it could launch the Sludge Bomb, Pikachu's Thunderbolt struck, catching Arbok mid-air before it could dodge, electrocuting it with intense crackling.

Boom!

The Thunderbolt exploded, sending Arbok flying.

"Thunder!" Red's offense was swift and relentless, completely different from the earlier battle with Blastoise.

Of course, Pikachu was all about high-speed, fast attacks.

Pikachu's low growl rumbled as it sped towards Arbok, golden lightning crackling once more.

A massive bolt of lightning descended, striking down towards Arbok.

An Electric-type Thunder attack!

"Dig!"

Dylan quickly ordered Arbok.

If Pikachu continued its onslaught, another Thunderbolt after this Thunder would surely knock Arbok out.

Arbok, gritting its teeth against the pain of the earlier Thunderbolt, plunged into the ground.

The Thunder attack hit the ground with explosive force, but it was just a moment too late.

"Pikachu, stay alert and observe," Red's red eyes remained calm and focused.

Pikachu didn't have a great way to counter Dig. It could try using Thunderbolt at the hole's entrance, but what if Arbok was waiting right there?

That would be too risky. It was better to wait and watch, and Red trusted his Pikachu's ability to react quickly to avoid a Dig attack.

"He didn't go for a follow-up, how cautious," Dylan noted with a hint of disappointment. If Red had commanded Pikachu to use Thunderbolt at the hole, it would've been perfect.

Yes, Arbok was indeed lying in wait at the hole.

But no matter.

"Toxic Spikes!" With Blastoise out of the picture, there was no need to worry about Toxic Spikes.

"Hmm?" Red frowned slightly.

From the hole, Arbok's head emerged, a deep purple Toxic Spikes ball forming at its mouth.

"Pikachu! Thunderbolt!"

"Pika~!"

Pikachu acted quickly, launching a swift Thunderbolt at Arbok.

Boom!

Thunderbolt struck Arbok once again, causing it to cry out in pain, its condition deteriorating rapidly.

But the Toxic Spikes had already been released into the air.

"Electro Ball!"

Pikachu leaped into the air, sending another crackling Electro Ball hurtling toward Arbok.

Boom!

The Electro Ball exploded, sending up a cloud of dust.

High above, the Toxic Spikes burst like fireworks, scattering strange purple fluid that seeped into the ground.

The entire battlefield seemed to glow with an eerie purple light before returning to normal.

When the dust settled, Arbok lay at the entrance of the hole, its eyes swirling, unconscious.

Red furrowed his brow, recalling what he knew about Toxic Spikes.

"Arbok is unable to battle. Pikachu wins," Leah, acting as the referee, did her job well. She quickly checked on Arbok, confirmed its condition, and announced the result.

"Yay~ Pikachu is amazing! We scored the first point!" Ash cheered excitedly. Even though Blastoise had been heavily injured, it was a great start for Pikachu.
